### [1] [Hyponormality of Generalized Cesàro Matrices of Every Positive Integer Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00193)
*Yicen Ma*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文对广义Cesàro矩阵（$\ell^2(\mathbb N_0)$上，元素为 $m(n-j+1)_{m-1}/(n+\alpha+1)_m$，$j\le n$）给出了计算机辅助证明：该算子为亚正规当且仅当 $\alpha\ge0$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定广义Cesàro矩阵在 $\ell^2(\mathbb N_0)$ 上的亚正规性的充要参数条件，并给出不依赖于参数采样或无限算子截断的严格证明方法。

Method: 核心代数手段是将辅助缺陷算子显式分解为一个正对角算子与至多 $m$ 个秩一算子之和，其符号与系数由离散Gram多项式确定；对非负参数用加权估计将正定性约化为标量不等式，并以解析估计覆盖所有 $m\ge32768$，其余阶数与整个参数区间用有限有理证书覆盖。

Result: 证明了该算子亚正规当且仅当 $\alpha\ge0$；证明还给出自交换子的统一加权下界；当 $-1<\alpha<0$ 时，有限支撑向量对每个阶数均给出负二次型。

Conclusion: 该工作以“显式缺陷算子分解 + 加权标量不等式 + 计算机辅助有理证书”的方式，完整刻画了广义Cesàro矩阵的亚正规性，并给出了自交换子的定量下界。

Abstract: For a positive integer $m$ and a real parameter $α>-1$, consider the generalized Cesàro matrix on $\ell^2(\mathbb N_0)$ with entries $m(n-j+1)_{m-1}/(n+α+1)_m$ for $j\le n$. We give a computer-assisted proof that this operator is hyponormal if and only if $α\ge0$. The main algebraic ingredient is an explicit decomposition of an auxiliary defect operator into a positive diagonal operator and at most $m$ rank-one operators. Discrete Gram polynomials determine the signs and coefficients of these terms. For nonnegative parameters, weighted estimates reduce positivity to scalar inequalities. A uniform analytic estimate covers every $m\ge32768$, while finite rational certificates cover the remaining orders and entire parameter intervals, without parameter sampling or truncating an infinite operator. The proof also gives a uniform weighted lower bound for the self-commutator. For $-1<α<0$, finite-support vectors give negative quadratic forms for every order.

</details>


### [2] [The Hilbert matrix operator into the Bergman space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00252)
*David Norrbo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明Hilbert矩阵算子 $H:A^2_{\ln^\alpha}\to A^2$ 在 $\alpha\geq 2$ 时有界，$\alpha<2$ 时无界，解决了一个公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Karapetrović 与 Jevtić 提出了 Hilbert 矩阵在加权对数Bergman空间上的有界性是否成立这一问题，本文旨在解决该开放问题，并确定临界指标。

Method: 证明更一般的加权序列空间结果：当 $v(n)=(\ln n)^p/n$、$w(n)=1/n$ 时，$H:\ell^p_v\to\ell^p_w$ 有界；再由该结论推导出 $\alpha=2$ 时 $H:A^2_{\ln^\alpha}\to A^2$ 的有界性（结合 $\alpha\geq 2$ 的单调解），并用反例/构造说明 $\alpha<2$ 时映射不进入 $A^2$。

Result: $\alpha\geq 2$ 时 $H:A^2_{\ln^\alpha}\to A^2$ 有界；$\alpha<2$ 时 $H$ 不将 $A^2_{\ln^\alpha}$ 映入 $A^2$；作为推论得到 $\alpha=2$ 时的有界性，临界指数为 $\alpha=2$。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了对数加权 Bergman 空间上 Hilbert 矩阵算子的有界性阈值，回答并解决了 Karapetrović–Jevtić 的公开问题。若需进一步分析，请补充作者的定理细节或证明。

Abstract: We prove that the Hilbert matrix operator $H:A^2_{\ln^α} \to A^2$ is bounded when $α\geq 2$ and that $H$ does not map $A^2_{\ln^α} $ into $A^2$ when $α<2$, solving an open problem posed by B. Karapetrović and M. Jevtić. The boundedness when $α=2$ is shown as a corollary to the more general statement: the Hilbert matrix $H:\ell_v^p \to \ell_w^p$ is bounded, where $v(n) = (\ln n)^p / n$ and $w(n) = 1/n$.

</details>


### [3] [Self-improvement for absolutely Cesáro bounded operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00271)
*Loris Arnold*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究绝对Cesàro有界算子的q-绝对Cesàro有界性、谱性质与多项式增长界，证明p-绝对Cesàro有界可推出更广范围的q-绝对Cesàro有界且范围最优，并在type s空间、L^p空间及ℓ^1上给出增长估计与反例构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画绝对Cesàro有界算子在更广p、q参数下的有界性传递关系，明确最佳可行范围，并探讨type条件对增长速率及谱结构的影响。

Method: 利用加权移位算例验证最优性；在type s∈(1,2]的Banach空间上通过插值与对数估计推导q-绝对Cesàro有界；在L^p空间上将结论从正算子推广到个体最终正算子；通过构造ℓ^1上的算子给出谱与极限上界反例。

Result: 证明p-绝对Cesàro有界（常数K>1）蕴含q-绝对Cesàro有界，1≤q<pK/(K-1)，且该范围最优（甚至对正加权移位成立）；type s空间上绝对Cesàro有界蕴含q-绝对Cesàro有界（1≤q<s），q=s处有对数估计，并得到‖T^n‖=O(n^{1/s-ε})；L^p空间上推广至个体最终正算子；ℓ^1上构造出p-绝对Cesàro有界T，σ(T)⊆D∪{1}但limsup‖T^n(I-T)‖=∞，且同一算子对所有p均p-绝对Cesàro有界。

Conclusion: 绝对Cesàro有界性的q-推广具有最优参数范围，并受空间type约束；即使谱位于单位圆盘并含点1，仍可能出现T^n(I-T)范数无界，说明谱条件不足以控制该增长。

Abstract: We prove that every $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded operator with constant $K>1$ is $q$-absolutely Cesàro bounded for $1\leq q<pK/(K-1)$. This range is optimal, even for positive weighted shifts. On Banach spaces of type $s\in (1,2]$, we prove that every absolutely Cesàro bounded operator is $q$-absolutely Cesàro bounded for every $1\leq q<s$, with a logarithmic estimate at $q=s$. We also prove the polynomial growth bound $\| T^n \| =O(n^{1/s-\varepsilon})$ for some $\varepsilon>0$. On $L^p$-spaces, $1<p<\infty$, we extend the implication from absolute Cesàro boundedness to $p$-absolute Cesàro boundedness from positive to individually eventually positive operators. Finally, for every $1\leq p<\infty$, we construct a $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded operator $T$ on $\ell^1$ such that $σ(T)\subseteq\mathbb D\cup\{1\}$ but $\limsup_{n\to\infty}\|T^n(I-T)\|=\infty$. We also observe that the construction yields a single operator which is $p$-absolutely Cesàro bounded for every $1\leq p<\infty$.

</details>


### [4] [Dilation Theory for Scaled \(q\)-Numerical Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00443)
*Matias Kaunisto,Ryan O'Loughlin,Jani Virtanen*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文将Berger膨胀定理从数值域推广到scaled q-数值域，证明若W_q(A)/q含于闭单位圆盘，则A存在酉ρ_q-膨胀，并给出ρ_q关于|q|的显式表达式，进而推出包含Ω_q(A)的圆盘为完全ρ_q-谱集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Berger膨胀定理刻画了数值域与完全谱集之间的联系，作者希望把这一理论框架扩展到q-数值域（scaled q-numerical range）的情形，以获得更一般的膨胀常数与谱集结论。

Method: 通过定义scaled q-数值域W_q(A)/q及其关联集合Ω_q(A)，在算子膨胀理论框架下构造酉ρ_q-膨胀，并确定膨胀常数ρ_q=max{1, 2|q|/(1+√(1-|q|^2))}。

Result: 当W_q(A)/q包含于闭单位圆盘时，A存在酉ρ_q-膨胀；由此得到包含Ω_q(A)的任意圆盘都是A的完全ρ_q-谱集。取q=1时ρ_q=2，恢复经典结论：包含W(A)的圆盘是完全2-谱集。

Conclusion: 该工作为q-数值域提供了统一的膨胀与谱集理论，经典Berger定理是其q=1特例，膨胀常数随|q|减小可低于2。

Abstract: We generalise Berger's dilation theorem from numerical ranges to scaled $q$-numerical ranges. Specifically, we prove that if the scaled $q$-numerical range $\frac{W_q(A)}{q}$ is contained in the closed unit disk, then $A$ admits a unitary $ρ_q$-dilation, where \[ ρ_q=\max\left\{1,\frac{2|q|}{1+\sqrt{1-|q|^2}}\right\}. \] As a corollary we show that every disk containing $Ω_q(A)$ is a complete $ρ_q$-spectral set for $A$. At $q=1$, this recovers the classical fact that any disk containing $W(A)$ is a complete $2$-spectral set for $A$.

</details>


### [5] [The Separable Quotient Problem and the Lotz Property in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00584)
*Gustavo Araújo,Anderson Barbosa,Juan B. Seoane-Sepúlveda*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文建立了可分离商问题（SQP）的若干新的充分或等价条件，并证明每个不具备Lotz性质的无限维Banach空间都含有可分离商，从而揭示了SQP与半群理论之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可分离商问题（SQP）是Banach空间理论中长期未决的经典问题，即每个无限维Banach空间是否都存在可分离商空间。作者希望给出新的充分或等价刻画，并从新的理论视角探索该问题。

Method: 通过建立若干新的充分条件或等价条件来刻画SQP；并引入"Lotz性质"这一概念，分析不具备该性质的无限维Banach空间的可分离商存在性，从而将SQP与半群理论相关联。

Result: 获得了SQP的若干新的充分或等价条件；证明了每个不具备Lotz性质的无限维Banach空间都含有可分离商；由此揭示了SQP与半群理论之间出人意料的联系。

Conclusion: SQP可通过多种等价/充分条件刻画，且缺乏Lotz性质的空间均满足SQP，表明该问题与半群理论存在深刻关联，为后续研究提供了新的思路与工具。

Abstract: In this paper, in addition to establishing several new sufficient or equivalent conditions for the well-known Separable Quotient Problem (SQP), we show that every infinite-dimensional Banach space lacking the Lotz property admits a separable quotient, thereby revealing a surprising connection between the SQP and semigroup theory.

</details>


### [6] [Riesz transform characterization of Hardy spaces associated with Dunkl--Schrödinger operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00703)
*Agnieszka Hejna-Łyżwa*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了由$-L$生成半群极大算子定义的Hardy空间$H_L^1$，证明其在势函数局部积分条件与半群衰减条件下可由Riesz变换的$L^1(dw)$范数等价刻画，适用于任意根系与非负重数函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dunkl Laplacian相关的Schrödinger算子$L=-\Delta_k+V$对应的Hardy空间$H_L^1$。经典情形下Hardy空间可由Riesz变换刻画，但在带势的Dunkl框架下，需要在适当的势函数与半群条件下建立类似的等价刻画。

Method: 将$H_L^1$的原子刻画与局部Hardy空间理论相结合，并利用Dunkl热核的Dunkl导数的加权积分估计。条件包括势函数的局部积分条件以及相对于立方体容许覆盖表述的半群衰减条件。

Result: 在势函数局部积分条件与半群衰减条件下，证明了\[
  \|f\|_{H_L^1}
  \asymp
  \|f\|_{L^1(dw)}
  +\sum_{j=1}^N
  \|\widetilde{R}_j f\|_{L^1(dw)}.
\]即$H_L^1$范数与$f$及Riesz变换$\widetilde{R}_j f$的$L^1(dw)$范数之和等价。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了Dunkl型Schrödinger算子Hardy空间的Riesz变换刻画，且适用于任意根系与非负重数函数，推广了相关经典结果。

Abstract: Let $L=-Δ_k+V$ be a Schrödinger operator associated with the Dunkl Laplacian and a nonnegative potential. We characterize the Hardy space $H_L^1$, defined by the maximal operator of the semigroup generated by $-L$, in terms of the Riesz transforms associated with $L$. Under a local integral condition on the potential and a decay condition on the semigroup, formulated relative to an admissible covering by cubes, we prove that \[
  \|f\|_{H_L^1}
  \asymp
  \|f\|_{L^1(dw)}
  +\sum_{j=1}^N
  \|\widetilde{R}_j f\|_{L^1(dw)}. \] The proof combines an atomic characterization of $H_L^1$ with local Hardy space theory and weighted integral estimates for Dunkl derivatives of the heat kernels. The result applies to arbitrary root systems and nonnegative multiplicity functions.

</details>


### [7] [On the Shalit-Shamovich Spectral Radius](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00816)
*Maximilian Tornes*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: Shalit 与 Shamovich 引入的谱半径唯一决定算子空间结构：若两算子空间结构对应的谱半径在每个 d 元算子组上一致，则恒等映射完全等距。这一结果回答了 Scherer、Shalit 与 Shamovich 的问题，并给出了最小谱半径的类似结论，以及对算子空间单位球上有界非交换函数代数的完全有界同构问题的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于确定谱半径这一数值不变量是否足以刻画算子空间结构，即回答 Scherer、Shalit 与 Shamovich 提出的问题。

Method: 通过比较定义在 C^d 上的两个算子空间结构 E_1 与 E_2 所对应的谱半径 ρ_{E_1} 与 ρ_{E_2}，证明当二者在所有 d 元算子组上取值一致时，恒等映射完全等距；并将方法推广至最小谱半径的情形。

Result: 证明了谱半径唯一决定算子空间结构，获得最小谱半径的类似结论，并给出在算子空间单位球上有界非交换函数代数的完全有界同构问题中的应用。

Conclusion: 谱半径（及最小谱半径）是完全刻画算子空间结构的充分不变量，为完全有界同构问题提供了新的工具与应用。

Abstract: We show that the spectral radius introduced by Shalit and Shamovich uniquely determines the underlying operator space structure: If E_1 and E_2 are operator space structures on C^d such that the corresponding spectral radii ρ_{E_1} and ρ_{E_2} agree on every d-tuple of operators, then the identity map is completely isometric. This answers a question of Scherer, Shalit and Shamovich. Moreover, we obtain an analogous result for the minimal spectral radii, and showcase an application to the completely bounded isomorphism problem for algebras of bounded non-commutative functions on operator space unit balls.

</details>

*Kai Fang,Yi Gao,Jinghao Huang,Fedor Sukochev*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文刻画了Marcinkiewicz空间（正）单位球的端点，并以此证明非交换Marcinkiewicz空间上的正等距都是初等形式；在特定原子von Neumann代数上，正满等距当且仅当为保持奇异值函数的Jordan *-同构的限制。此外还研究了弱L_p空间、Marcinkiewicz序列空间、弱ℓ_p算子理想上的满等距及单参数等距群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在推广非交换L_p空间等距刻画的相关结果到Marcinkiewicz空间与弱型空间，通过端点刻画这一经典工具来分类这些空间上的等距映射及其结构。

Method: 先刻画Marcinkiewicz空间在自然拟范数下（正）单位球的端点；利用端点结果分析正等距的形式；通过奇异值函数与Jordan *-同构来刻画原子von Neumann代数上的正满等距；并对弱L_p空间、Marcinkiewicz序列空间、弱ℓ_p算子理想上的（不一定正的）满等距及单参数等距群进行研究。

Result: 证明了非交换Marcinkiewicz空间上的每个正等距都是初等形式；在原子具相同迹的原子von Neumann代数上，线性映射为正满等距当且仅当它是保持奇异值函数的Jordan *-同构的限制；并给出了弱L_p空间、Marcinkiewicz序列空间、弱ℓ_p算子理想上满等距及单参数等距群的相应结论。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Marcinkiewicz空间的端点理论，并将等距分类结果统一扩展到非交换与弱型算子空间框架。

Abstract: We characterize extreme points of the (positive) unit ball of Marcinkiewicz spaces with respect to their natural quasi-norms. Having these results at hand, we show that every positive isometry on a noncommutative Marcinkiewicz space is of elementary form. In particular, a linear mapping on a noncommutative Marcinkiewicz space over an atomic von Neumann algebra with all atoms having the same trace is a positive surjective isometry if and only if it is the restriction of a Jordan $*$-isomorphism which preserves the singular value functions. We also study (not necessarily positive) surjective isometries on weak $L_p$-spaces affiliated a $σ$-finite non-atomic von Neumann algebra for $1<p<\infty$, Marcinkiewicz sequence spaces, and weak $\ell_p$ operator ideals, $p>0$, as well as one-parameter groups of isometries on the latter two.

</details>

Main category: math.FA

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定齐次多项式在ℓ_{p_1}^n×⋯×ℓ_{p_m}^n上m线性型最小范数关于n的精确增长幂次，并补齐系数范数与上确界范数比较问题在2≤p≤m、2≤r≤∞情形下缺失的多项式增长指数。

Conclusion: 稀疏系数估计与加权Hardy–Littlewood分析相互印证，给出精确范数增长指数和最优权指数，并确定相关极值问题的维数障碍。

Abstract: We study sparse Kahane--Salem--Zygmund constructions and weighted Hardy--Littlewood inequalities for homogeneous polynomials. For supports of cardinality $n^{d+o(1)}$, we determine the sharp power of $n$ governing the smallest norm of a unimodular $m$-linear form on $\ell_{p_1}^n\times\cdots\times\ell_{p_m}^n$; in the diagonal case, this yields the missing polynomial growth exponent in the coefficient-versus-supremum norm problem for $2\le p\le m$ and $2\le r\le\infty$. We then introduce a diagonal weighted Hardy--Littlewood functional which, on $s=p\ge m$, agrees exactly with the classical Hardy--Littlewood coefficient norm with the same optimal constant. We determine the optimal diagonal weight exponent for $2\le p\le m$ and $1\le q\le2$, on the full critical line $p=m$, and on a sharp part of the region $q>2$; at $q=\infty$ the optimal weight exponent is obtained for every $2\le p\le m$. The sparse coefficient estimates provide the matching dimensional obstructions.

</details>


### [10] [Diagonal operators on Janson-Sobolev and Janson-Sobolev-Hardy spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01628)
*Krystian Kazaniecki,Richard Lechner*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Janson-Sobolev与Janson-Sobolev-Hardy空间的Banach空间及算子分解结构，证明了由q-adic滤子、子空间V与重排不变函数空间X确定的鞅空间上，特定对角算子几乎可投影分解通过D，常数趋于1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Janson-Sobolev与Janson-Sobolev-Hardy空间是一类新的鞅空间，其Banach空间结构与算子分解性质尚待刻画；作者希望建立该类空间的算子分解理论，并给出对角算子的分解结果。

Abstract: We study the Banach space and operator factorization structure of Janson-Sobolev and Janson-Sobolev-Hardy spaces. Abstract: Our study connects root-mean-square ballistic transport to potential-theoretic recurrence for finite-range open quantum random walks. In homogeneous walks with primitive local channels, finitely many simple periodic Fourier peripheral eigenvalues, no nonzero stationary Fourier mode, and a nondegenerate quadratic spectral term, we prove a periodic uniform local limit theorem and recover exponential finite-set return bounds for nonzero drift. The centered case yields strong Green-function asymptotics in at least three dimensions, as well as potential-kernel asymptotics in one and two dimensions. According to these assumptions, the RMS ballistic speed equals the drift norm, nonzero drift indicates transience, and centered walks are recurring in effective dimensions one and two but transitory in higher dimensions. The low-dimensional finding shows a recurrence of the origin projection in TOM. For reducible walks, an explicit harmonic \(h\)-transform converts each absorption component to an OQRW, providing a detailed breakdown of Green occupation potentials. The squared RMS speed is the absorption-weighted mean of squared component drifts, but the reduced drift-dimension classification also needs specific component return estimations. A centered noncommuting family validates the fundamental spectral assumptions in all dimensions and provides explicit potential constants. Exact finite traps and sparse reflecting barriers provide a complementary nonhomogeneous method for zero speed and TOM recurrence.

</details>

Abstract: We study combinatorial Banach spaces, i.e. Banach spaces induced by families of finite sets, of uncountable density. We prove some general theorems, for example we characterize when such spaces contain isomorphic copies of long $c_0$ and long $\ell_1$. We discuss the existence of Banach spaces which are (complementedly) universal in the category of Banach spaces with unconditional basis of fixed cardinality. We present some examples, in particular we show that the Banach spaces generated by Suslin trees do not contain uncountable equilateral sets.

</details>

### [14] [Detecting Einstein metrics on products of spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00066)
*Anna Siffert*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在 \(S^5\times S^2\)、\(S^5\times S^3\)、\(S^5\times S^4\)、\(S^7\times S^2\) 上构造出无穷多个两两非等距的实解析 Einstein 度量，且除有限个外均不等距或位似于 Riemann 乘积或经典双和 Böhm 度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在给定乘积流形上寻找大量非平凡、非乘积、非经典 Böhm 型的 Einstein 度量，拓展高维 Einstein 度量的存在性与多样性。

Method: 考虑形式为 \(dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}\)（\(p,q\ge2,\ 2p+q\le8\)）的共形/余齐性度量。从某一奇异轨道处两个非塌缩半径极小的解出发，放大该区域，使 Einstein 方程趋近 Ricci-平坦问题，其解以衰减振荡趋近锥。关键是从 Ricci-平坦极限中提取两个响应量：一个决定共同振荡相位，去掉该相位后将另一闭合条件化为标量符号变化；再用一致的内-外匹配把极限符号变化转移到精确 Einstein 方程。

Result: 随初始尺度趋于零，相位可任意多圈旋转，闭合条件因此被无穷多次满足，从而得到无穷多个 pairwise 非等距的实解析 Einstein 度量；除有限个外，它们不是 Riemann 乘积，也不是经典双和 Böhm 度量。

Conclusion: 通过 Ricci-平坦极限的振荡相位分析与内-外匹配，可在上述四类乘积流形上系统生成无穷多非平凡 Einstein 度量。

Abstract: On \[ S^5\times S^2,\qquad S^5\times S^3,\qquad S^5\times S^4,\qquad S^7\times S^2, \] we construct infinitely many pairwise non-isometric real-analytic Einstein metrics. After discarding finitely many members, none is isometric or homothetic to a Riemannian product or to a classical two-summand Böhm metric. The metrics have the form \[ dt^2+a(t)^2g_{S^p}+b(t)^2g_{S^p}+c(t)^2g_{S^q}, \qquad p,q\ge2,\qquad 2p+q\le8. \]
  The construction starts with solutions for which the two non-collapsing radii at one singular orbit are very small. After magnifying this region, the Einstein equations approach a Ricci-flat problem whose solutions tend to a cone by a decaying oscillation. The main point is to determine exactly which part of this oscillation can still be seen by the closing conditions at the other end of the manifold. Rather than following the full, increasingly oscillatory shooting map, we extract two response quantities from the Ricci-flat limit. One determines the common oscillatory phase; after that phase has been removed, the second closing condition is reduced to a scalar sign change. A uniform inner--outer matching argument transfers this limiting sign change to the exact Einstein equations. As the initial scale tends to zero, the phase makes arbitrarily many turns, and the closing conditions are therefore met infinitely many times.

</details>

Consequently, the PTR tangency spectrum is encoded by intrinsic soliton data together with the second fundamental form. For compact minimal PTR solitons with positive ambient curvature we obtain $λ\le c(n-1)/4$, with a strict version under a relative-nullity condition. We also prove compact triviality criteria, an exact trace-free anisotropy identity, a parallel-data rigidity result, and a nonexistence theorem for compact real hypersurfaces. A flat multi-level Gaussian model and a nonflat Segre $CR$-product provide explicit consistency checks and identify the limiting cases of the reconstruction formulas.

If $(dη)_p=0$, then \[ φA+Aφ=2G_N,\qquad G_NX=G(X,N), \] which forces the Hopf condition. On the contact hyperplane $\mathcal D_p=\kerη_p$, the endomorphism $φA+Aφ$ then commutes with $φ$, whereas $G_N$ anti-commutes with $φ$ and satisfies \[ G_N^2=-αI_{\mathcal D_p},\qquad α>0. \] This contradiction proves the obstruction. As a connection-theoretic consequence, for the standard Yano semi-symmetric metric connection associated with $ξ$, \[ \widehat\nabla_XY=\nabla_XY+η(Y)X-g(X,Y)ξ, \] one has in dimension five \[ \operatorname{Skew}\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}=-\frac32\,dη, \] and hence $\operatorname{Ric}^{\widehat\nabla}$ is nowhere symmetric. Abstract: Yau asked whether every complete Riemannian manifold with bounded Ricci curvature and positive injectivity radius admits an isometric embedding into some Euclidean space with bounded mean curvature. The Gauss equation then forces every $C^2$ isometric immersion of this metric into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space to have unbounded mean curvature. The metric is assembled from disjoint curvature peaks whose Ricci contractions stay uniformly bounded. The construction works in every dimension at least four, and in dimension four the Ricci curvature may be chosen to tend to zero at infinity.

Conclusion: 该研究通过统一的实解析延拓与焦点迹方法，在无需完备性或holonomicity条件下解决了Chen猜想（欧几里得余维一）和双调和极小性问题，并刻画了谱极小性的尖锐范围；同时建立了非极小测地球与Clifford超曲面的定量局部刚性。适用范围涵盖广泛的非线性平均曲率谱方程。

Abstract: We study nonlinear mean-curvature spectral equations for hypersurfaces in Euclidean, spherical and hyperbolic space. For an admissible analytic response $F$ and ambient sectional curvature $c$, we prove minimality when the spectral parameter satisfies $σ\ge nc$, and strict mean-curvature bounds at gradient points when $σ<nc$. The class includes every power $F(t)=t^{p-1}$, $p>1$, and positive mixtures satisfying an explicit width condition. In particular, the result proves Chen's conjecture in Euclidean codimension one and biharmonic minimality in constant negative curvature, without completeness or holonomicity. A common real-analytic continuation and focal-trace argument treats all three geometries. Explicit umbilical models show that the unconditional spectral minimality range is sharp. Independently, we prove quantitative local rigidity, modulo ambient isometries, of compact nonminimal geodesic spheres in all three geometries and proper equal-radius Clifford hypersurfaces in spheres, for smooth laws with $F,F'>0$ near the model amplitude.

</details>

Abstract: Smyth (1984) proved that every tetrahedron $T\subset\mathbb{R}^3$ contains three embedded minimal disks meeting $\partial T$ orthogonally, obtained as conjugate surfaces of Plateau solutions in a skew quadrilateral $Q$ whose edges are $c_iν_i$, where $\varphi_1,\varphi_2,\varphi_3,\varphi_4$ are faces of $T$, $c_i=\operatorname{Area}(\varphi_i)$ and $ν_i$ are outward normal vectors. For the $\textit{regular}$ tetrahedron we give the Weierstrass data explicitly. The Gauss map is a Schwarz triangle function for a circular-arc triangle with angles $(π/4,\ π/2,\ πβ/2)$, where $β=\arccos(-1/3)/π$; the hypergeometric parameters are linear in $β$ and there is $\textit{no accessory parameter and no free parameter}$. We record a closed form for $κ$ in $Γ$-values, a Wronskian closed form for $g'$, the exact total curvature $2π(2β-1)$, the symmetric-square structure of the data, and a rigidity statement: since $β/2$ is irrational, the projective monodromy is dense and the data $\textit{cannot}$ be algebraic. Numerically the representation extends to 28 digits -- $\operatorname{Area}/\ell^{2}=0.2172341554075574483760009126\ldots$ -- confirming a 2013 Surface Evolver computation of Ken Brakke.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 圆周的Wasserstein空间非平坦，其曲率结构由辛形式刻画且无界；本文纠正了关于一维底流形Wasserstein空间平坦的普遍预期，并建立了基于Laplacian特征函数的整体标架工具，随后将其应用于动力系统中扩张圆周映射的推前作用导数的计算，将其读作Fourier频率上的加权移位。

Abstract: We show that the Wasserstein space of the circle is not flat, in contrast with the flatness of the Wasserstein spaces of the real line and of an interval. Its sectional curvature is governed by a symplectic form on the Fourier coefficients, so that a plane is flat exactly when it is isotropic for that form, and on the plane spanned by the $n$-th Fourier modes the curvature is bounded above by $3n^2$, with equality exactly at the uniform measure. It is therefore unbounded. We compute it inside the formal Riemannian structure of Otto and confirm it directly from the definition of $W_2$, which also shows that no neighbourhood of the uniform measure in $P_2(S^1)$ is CAT(0). The result corrects the widely stated expectation that a Wasserstein space is flat whenever its base manifold is one-dimensional.
  The computation is carried out in the global frame of the tangent bundle of $P^\infty(M)$ induced by the eigenfunctions of the Laplacian of $M$, whose existence was observed by Lott. We prove that it is a Schauder basis of every tangent space and write Otto's metric and its Levi-Civita connection in it. On the circle this gives the metric and connection coefficients of $P^\infty(S^1)$ in closed form, as finite combinations of the Fourier coefficients of the density. A final section puts the frame to work on a computation from dynamics, reading the derivative of the push-forward action of an expanding circle map at its invariant measure as a weighted shift on the Fourier frequencies.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we construct explicit formulas for the heat kernel of the Hodge Laplacian restricted to an invariant subspace of $1$-forms on the real hyperbolic space $\mathbb{H}^{n}$. Using warped product decompositions, the problem is reduced to component operators on specific subspaces, yielding precise closed-form expressions for both odd and even dimensions. We further establish Millson-type recurrence relations that connect heat kernels across differing dimensions. Based on these explicit representations, we derive sharp uniform estimates and long-time asymptotic behavior, extending classical scalar heat kernel bounds to $1$-forms on real hyperbolic spaces.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a geometric way to uniformly carry out Hörmander's $L^2$-estimates for Hermitian-Yang-Mills connections with bounded energy on Kähler surfaces. Abstract: Following Miyamoto, we consider Q-charts, a class of subductions $φ\colon X\to Y$ of diffeological spaces along which Blohmann's elasticity descends. More precisely, structures on $Y$ correspond bijectively to structures on $X$ that are invariant under the pseudogroup $Ψ(φ)$ of fibre-preserving transitions, and torsion-freeness, effectivity, flatness, fullness, the Levi-Civita property and the geodesic equation are preserved in both directions. The key input is that the iterated tangent bundles $T^nX$ and their fibre products $T_kX$ are the pullbacks along $φ$ of the corresponding bundles over $Y$.

As an application, we determine the Riemannian geometry of the irrational tori $\Tor_α=\R/(\Z+α\Z)$ completely. Let $\vartheta$ be the one-form on $\Tor_α$ induced by $dt$ on $\R$. Every Riemannian metric is a positive constant multiple of $\vartheta^2$, and the vertical connections form a one-parameter family of flat effective connections, a single member of which is the unique Levi-Civita connection of every metric. This connection has a complete geodesic flow, yet the induced pseudo-distance vanishes identically.

Using this local quasireflection, we modify classical estimates of Ahlfors and show that domains bounded by asymptotically conformal curves have asymptotically similar hyperbolic and quasihyperbolic metrics. Furthermore, by combining these estimates with a geometric reinterpretation of the extension through Epstein surfaces, we show that the Epstein-Poincaré surfaces of the complementary domains are arbitrarily close to one another near the curve.

Abstract: We prove global strong well-posedness for the normalized isothermal, isobaric ternary Maxwell-Stefan diffusion system on bounded $C^{2+α_0}$ domains, for arbitrary positive constant binary frictions and compatible nonnegative initial data, without a smallness condition. For distinct frictions, trace--determinant variables produce two separated spectral roots satisfying scalar parabolic equations with antisymmetric quadratic coupling. Euler-Poisson-Darboux one-sided truncations, stabilized by the mixing entropy, yield boundary De Giorgi oscillation decay and positive-time Hölder regularity. The mixing-entropy Hessian then provides a local symmetrizer; conormal Schauder estimates give uniform $C^{1+a}$ control, and frozen maximal $L_p$-regularity yields a uniform terminal trace bound excluding finite-time breakdown. Repeated frictions reduce to triangular scalar parabolic systems. Every component with positive conserved mass is strictly positive for positive time.

Method: 基于Stieltjes坐标变换与De Giorgi正则性理论：证明全局最大-$L^p$强适定性；刻画保持固定Stieltjes特征标架的最大Darken类；对一般光滑一致正Darken示踪扩散率导出直接约束通量形式、精确混合熵对称化、Fisher耗散、组分一致法向椭圆性与局部强适定性；用等因子分组处理常数乘性摩擦。

Abstract: The core-diagonal closure of Bothe and Druet identifies multicomponent Darken diffusion as a thermodynamically consistent Maxwell-Stefan model in which the constitutive data are species tracer diffusivities. Building on the Stieltjes-coordinate and De Giorgi regularity framework introduced in our recent preprint for additive Maxwell-Stefan frictions, we prove global maximal-$L^p$ strong well-posedness, without smallness assumptions, for the affine Darken class with composition-dependent scalar coefficients and uniformly positive tracer diffusivities. We show that this is exactly the maximal Darken class preserving the fixed Stieltjes eigenframe underlying the global argument. Solutions in the affine class remain uniformly separated from the simplex boundary, become classical for positive times, and converge exponentially to equilibrium. Abstract: We study an optimal control problem for the two-dimensional Navier-Stokes equations projected onto the tangent space of the $\mathbb{H}$-unit sphere manifold $\mathcal{M} := \{\boldsymbol{u} \in \mathbb{H} : \|\boldsymbol{u}\|_{\mathbb{H}}=1\}$. Furthermore, we establish the Fréchet differentiability of the control-to-state mapping $\boldsymbol{U}\mapsto \boldsymbol{u}_{\boldsymbol{U}}$ in $\mathrm{C}([0,T];\mathbb{H})\cap\mathrm{L}^2(0,T;\mathbb{V})$, with the corresponding linearized state characterized as the unique weak solution of a linearized constrained Navier-Stokes system preserving the tangency condition. These results provide a rigorous analytical foundation for the optimal control of fluid flows constrained to nonlinear manifolds.

Abstract: This article is the third in a series investigating ill-posedness in critical Sobolev spaces for Camassa-Holm-type equations. We prove ill-posedness at this endpoint, complementing the local well-posedness theory in $H^s(\mathbb R)$ for $s>3/2$ and the recently established norm-inflation result for $1<s<3/2$.

For $0<γ\leq3$, we construct arbitrarily small smooth initial data whose corresponding solutions develop finite-time blow-up, with the maximal lifespan tending to zero with the size of the initial data. Abstract: We study the relation between ground states, criticality, and variational compactness for a family of genuinely multipolar Lp-Hardy weights, 1 < p < n. Abstract: We summarize recent developments in the theory of the Benjamin-Ono equation, a well-known long-wave asymptotic model for internal waves in deep water, focusing on results obtained by means of an explicit formula for the solution of the Cauchy problem in terms of given initial data. We give a full description of this formula, and provide simple examples, with the aim of bringing it into the realm of applied mathematics. We also show how the formula simplifies further upon restriction to rational initial data. We then show how the formula and its rational restriction explain in great detail, and in a strikingly simple fashion, the asymptotic behavior of solutions of the Benjamin-Ono equation in the small-dispersion and long-time limits. In the setting of long-time asymptotics, we prove some new results related to soliton resolution yielding pointwise convergence with a concrete decay rate under the assumption of rational initial data. The first half of the paper is a stand-alone general survey and user's guide, while the appendices constituting the second half are intended for readers who want to explore the topics in greater depth, and it includes the proofs of our new results.

Abstract: We study finite-time blow-up and global existence for semilinear heat equations with time-dependent reaction coefficients on metric measure spaces. Under additional conservativity and heat kernel assumptions, we also prove blow-up at the critical Fujita exponent associated with polynomial volume growth. In particular, the global existence criterion only uses the behaviour of the nonlinearity on the range explored by the solution. Abstract: We study an incompressible visco-morphoelastic system in three dimensions in which the Eulerian effective strain is transported by the Zaremba-Jaumann rate and coupled to a Navier-Stokes momentum balance through a Kelvin-Voigt stress. The strain remains symmetric and splits into a scalar transport-relaxation equation for its trace and a deviatoric equation containing the nonlinear stretching and Jaumann terms. We regularize both equations by diffusion of order $\varepsilon$, which admits a mechanical interpretation as weakly nonlocal remodeling with a characteristic length scale. For fixed $\varepsilon>0$ we construct Leray-Hopf type weak solutions by a truncated Galerkin scheme and remove the truncation a posteriori using a maximum principle for the scalar variable. As $\varepsilon\to0$, the scalar variable converges strongly in $L^p((0,T)\timesΩ)$ for every $1\le p<\infty$, by a DiPerna-Lions commutator argument on bounded Lipschitz domains, allowing the stretching term to be identified in the limit. In contrast, the Jaumann commutator is a product of two weakly convergent sequences, and its limit cannot be identified from the available estimates. We therefore obtain a defect \[\boldsymbol{\mathcal{D}}\in L^2(0,T;(H^2(Ω;\mathbb{S}_0))^\ast)\] in the limiting deviatoric equation. We give two sufficient conditions for $\boldsymbol{\mathcal{D}}=0$. The energy-level cancellation used in related corotational viscoelastic models does not close here because the coefficient of the stretching term is itself an unknown.

</details>

Abstract: We establish a global uniqueness result at the critical Sobolev regularity for the isotropic Calderón problem in all dimensions $n\ge 4$.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 研究对象为 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中光滑边缘密度下的二次代价二次正则化最优传输，取正则化参数 $\varepsilon\to0$。技术路线包括支撑的局部几何分析（截面极限形状）、以 Brenier 像为中心并按 $\ell=\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$ 缩放后的渐近展开、对偶势的 $\ell^2$ 阶一致展开及其系数识别、以及由线性 Neumann 问题确定全局修正项；随后分析两种 Brenier 映射近似（对偶势梯度与耦合的条件均值），推导 $L^p$ 收敛率与常数，并区分内部与边界偏差。

Conclusion: 结果表明，二次正则化在小参数极限下能够对经典最优传输提供准确且稀疏的近似：其支撑恢复 Brenier 映射的图，几何与对偶量具有可显式描述的渐近结构，且对 Brenier 映射的自然估计达到尖锐收敛率。这为理解正则化最优传输的偏差来源（局部分量加 Neumann 问题刻定的全局修正）提供了定量框架。

Abstract: We study quadratically regularized optimal transport with quadratic cost in the regime of small regularization $\varepsilon$, where the support of the optimal coupling is sparse. For smooth marginal densities in $\mathbb{R}^d$, we show that the support contains the graph of the Brenier map. After centering by the Brenier image and rescaling by $\ell=\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$, the sections of the support have explicit ellipsoidal limits for $\varepsilon\to0$, except at boundary points, where the limits are half-space profiles. The optimal dual potentials admit expansions at order $\ell^2$, uniformly up to the boundary. We identify the leading coefficients, which consist of a common local profile and opposite global corrections determined by a linear Neumann problem. Finally, we analyze two approximations to the Brenier map, namely the gradient of the dual potential and the conditional mean of the coupling. We obtain the sharp $L^p$ rate $\ell^{1+1/p}$ with exact leading constants and further identify the leading interior and boundary biases. Taken together, our results illustrate that quadratic regularization induces an accurate sparse approximation of classical optimal transport.

</details>

Motivation: 半经典Dirac算子的长时间演化受复共振谱支配，需理解共振展开、能量衰减速率及扭曲预解界，特别是质量型扰动在非捕获能量窗口下的行为。

Result: 正能共振贡献大正时间、负能共振贡献大负时间；非捕获窗口下得到连续截断预解的多项式界与快速局部能量衰减；引入最大正片连接时间T_χ后，证明几何连续预解估计，对任意固定M低于共振自由深度常数得到显式局部能量衰减阈值T_N>T_χ+(N+1)/M；并给出全扭曲预解无法有一致[h log(1/h)]^{-1}界的严格微观局部障碍，识别出对数深度下夹层连续预解的有限飞行时间放大机制。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了半经典Dirac算子共振展开与局部长时间能量衰减，量化了衰减阈值与连接时间的关系，并指出扭曲预解在共振自由深度的本质限制。

Abstract: We study resonance expansions and localised long-time dynamics for three-dimensional semiclassical Dirac operators. For smooth Hermitian matrix-valued perturbations which are analytic outside a compact set, we first obtain exponential cut-off resolvent bounds away from the resonance set and polynomial bounds in thin resonance-free rectangles adjacent to the real axis. Combining these estimates with a local $\Oc(h^{-3})$ upper bound on the number of resonances, a pigeonhole selection of admissible contours, almost-analytic functional calculus, and a Cauchy--Green deformation, we derive resonance expansions for spectrally localised propagators. Positive-energy resonances contribute for large positive times, whereas negative-energy resonances contribute for large negative times. We then specialise to mass-type perturbations $\mbfβV(x)$ in a nontrapping energy window. Using the logarithmic resonance-free region established in our earlier work together with a quantitative FBI-transform/escape-function argument, we obtain a polynomial bound for the continued cut-off resolvent and deduce rapid local-energy decay. We subsequently refine this polynomial estimate by a finite-time propagation argument in the undistorted region. Introducing the maximal positive-sheet connection time $T_χ$ associated with the spatial cutoff, we prove a geometric continued-resolvent estimate and, for every fixed $M$ below the resonance-free depth constant, derive the explicit local-energy decay threshold $T_N>T_χ+(N+1)/M$. The same analysis gives a rigorous microlocal obstruction to a uniform $[h\log(1/h)]^{-1}$ bound for the full distorted resolvent and identifies a finite-flight-time amplification mechanism for the sandwiched continued resolvent at logarithmic depth.

</details>

Result: 证明自由边界由同心球面组成且唯一确定；给出半径随域、相互作用距离和边界数据的变化规律，特别证明当相互作用距离趋于零时自由边界收敛到相邻模型的自由边界；导出二维与$n\geq 3$维两种情形下自由边界半径的ODE，并给出若干算例与讨论。

Conclusion: 在环形域两种群特例中，自由边界几何结构被完全刻画为同心球面且唯一，半径随参数变化的依赖关系清晰，其演化可由ODE描述；结果将已有自由边界正则性理论在特定情形下推进为显式几何与定量描述。

Abstract: We consider a system of elliptic equations, depending on a small parameter $ε> 0$, which models the long range segregation of populations. The system has been previously studied in \cite{CL2} for the regularity of the free boundary in dimension $2$ and in \cite{ChPaTo26_2} and \cite{ChPaTo26_3} for the partial regularity of the free boundary in higher dimensions. In this paper, we consider the special case with $K = 2$ populations in an annular domain in arbitrary dimensions. Using the uniqueness and the rotational invariance of the solution, we show that the free boundary consist of concentric spheres. Moreover, by an application of the free boundary condition derived in \cite{CL2}, we show that the free boundary is uniquely determined. We also examine how the radius of the free boundary change according to the domain, the interaction distance and the boundary data. In particular, we show that the free boundary converges to that of the adjacent model as the interaction distance tends to zero.
  We also study the one parameter family of the elliptic system, in which the domain, the interaction distance, and the boundary data depends on $t$. We derive an ODE for the radius of the free boundary in dimension $2$ and dimension $n \geq 3$ separately. Several examples are given and discussed.

</details>

Result: 给出Besov分布的一个自然推广的Hörmander型乘法条件，并在整体波前集框架下证明该条件与相应的仿积分解之间存在对应关系，从而把分布乘法的微局部分析视角与Bony的仿积方法统一起来。

Conclusion: 该工作表明整体波前集框架为Besov分布乘法提供了与经典仿微分演算相容的微局部刻画，架起了Hörmander型可乘性理论与Bony仿积分解之间的桥梁。

Abstract: We establish a natural generalization of the Hörmander condition for the multiplication of Besov distributions using the global wavefront set framework of Coriasco, Johansson, and Toft. Using this condition, we relate the microlocal perspective on multiplication of distributions to the classical paraproduct decomposition of pointwise multiplication arising in the paradifferential calculus of Bony.

</details>

### [71] [A Counterexample to Wormald's Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00086)
*James Alexander Schreib*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出Wormald猜想的一个反例：一个16个顶点的三次图（$K_{3,3}$与一个由两个细分$K_4$构成的10顶点桥接图的非交并）无法将其边划分为两个同构的生成线性森林；通过拼接$K_4$可得到阶为$16+4t$的无穷族反例，并在Lean中无自定义公理地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wormald猜想是关于图的边分解的一个命题，本文旨在通过构造反例来否定该猜想，并为相关图的边划分问题提供新的见解。

Method: 构造一个具体的16顶点三次图（$K_{3,3}$与由两个细分$K_4$构成的10顶点桥接图的非交并），分析其边划分为两个同构生成线性森林的奇偶性障碍，即任何这样的划分都会迫使每个单色分量为偶数阶，同时要求桥的5顶点侧被划分为这样的分量；并通过拼接$K_4$生成无限族反例，使用Lean形式化验证。

Result: 该16顶点图不存在将边划分为两个同构生成线性森林的划分；证明障碍来自奇偶性；通过拼接$K_4$得到阶为$16+4t$的所有反例；该反例是非连通的，连通情形仍未解决；全族在Lean中无自定义公理地验证。

Conclusion: Wormald猜想被否定，存在无穷多个反例；但连通图的情形仍然悬而未决。

Abstract: We give a counterexample to Wormald's conjecture. A 16-vertex cubic graph, the disjoint union of $K_{3,3}$ with a 10-vertex bridged graph built from two subdivided copies of $K_4$, has no partition of its edges into two isomorphic spanning linear forests. The obstruction is parity: any such partition would force every monochromatic component to have even order while requiring a five-vertex side of a bridge to be partitioned into such components. Adjoining copies of $K_4$ gives counterexamples in every order $16+4t$. The counterexample is disconnected and leaves the connected case unresolved. The full family is verified in Lean without custom axioms.

</details>


### [72] [The Borowiecki-Broere Generalized Total Coloring Conjecture for Planar Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00091)
*James Alexander Schreib*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了Borowiecki与Broere的猜想：每个有限简单平面图都存在一种广义全着色，即用四种颜色的正常顶点着色配合四种颜色的边着色，且每个边色类都是森林，每条边不得与其任一端点同色。更强地，任意正常四着色都可扩展为所需的边着色。证明结合了平面图的组件计数不等式（通过补全为三角剖分得到）与应用于四个图拟阵的拟阵划分定理。文中还描述了相对于六个命名背景假设的Lean 4形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Borowiecki和Broere提出的关于有限简单平面图存在广义全着色的猜想，即用四种颜色实现正常顶点着色与边着色，且每个边色类为森林、边不能与其端点同色。

Method: 证明结合了两个关键工具：一是对顶点被划分为四个独立集的平面图，通过补全为三角剖分得到的组件计数不等式；二是将拟阵划分定理应用于四个图拟阵。此外，还描述了在六个命名背景假设下的Lean 4形式化。

Result: 每个有限简单平面图都存在所需的广义全着色，且任意正常四着色都可以扩展为所需的边着色，从而证明了Borowiecki-Broere猜想。

Conclusion: Borowiecki和Broere的猜想成立：有限简单平面图的任意正常四着色均可扩展为边着色，使每个边色类为森林且边不与端点同色。证明依赖组件计数不等式与拟阵划分定理，并给出了Lean 4形式化。

Abstract: We prove the conjecture of Borowiecki and Broere that every finite simple planar graph admits a generalized total coloring with four colors: a proper vertex coloring with four colors together with an edge coloring with four colors in which each edge color class is a forest and no edge receives the color of either endpoint. In fact every proper four-coloring of such a graph extends to an edge coloring of the required kind. The proof combines a component-count inequality for planar graphs whose vertices are partitioned into four independent sets, obtained by completing to a triangulation, with the matroid partition theorem applied to four graphic matroids. A Lean 4 formalization relative to six named background assumptions is described below.

</details>


### [73] [Periodic Joint Co-tiles of Independent Tiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00099)
*Hu Tan,Ying Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 对于 $\Z^d$ 中 $d-1$ 个独立有限瓦片，只要存在联合共瓦片，就存在完全周期的联合共瓦片，从而去掉了 Meyerovitch–Sanadhya–Solomon 周期存在定理中的性质 $(\star)$，并在 $d=2$ 时恢复 Bhattacharya 定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原周期存在定理依赖性质 $(\star)$ 这一额外假设，限制了结论的适用范围；本文旨在去掉该假设，证明对 $d-1$ 个独立有限瓦片，联合共瓦片的存在性自动蕴含完全周期联合共瓦片的存在性，并推广至更一般的有限族。

Method: 推广 Greenfeld–Tao 的布尔范式方法，构造平移轨道闭包中的构型，使其在有限指数格子的陪集上的限制要么在秩 $d-1$ 子群下不变，要么由密度为二分之一的仿射分数部分三项公式给出；这些限制通过铺砌方程配对形成有限二部图，再用二染色将相应限制替换为常数并保持所有铺砌方程。

Result: 证明了 $d-1$ 个独立有限瓦片在存在联合共瓦片时必存在完全周期联合共瓦片；更一般地，对指标函数为完全周期实函数加上有限多个在秩 $d-1$ 子群下不变的有界实函数的有限族，证明了周期存在性；$d=2$ 时恢复 Bhattacharya 定理。

Conclusion: 给出了用独立伴随族刻画周期可铺性的特征，以及对包含独立 $(d-1)$-子族的族在维度作为输入一部分时的判定算法。

Abstract: For every $d\geq2$, we prove that $d-1$ independent finite tiles in $\Z^d$ admit a fully periodic joint co-tile whenever they admit a joint co-tile. Here independence means that the tiles contain the origin and every choice of one nonzero vector from each tile is linearly independent. This removes property $(\star)$ from the periodic existence theorem of Meyerovitch, Sanadhya and Solomon and recovers Bhattacharya's theorem when $d=2$. More generally, we prove periodic existence for any finite family admitting a joint co-tile whose indicator is a fully periodic real function plus finitely many bounded real functions, each invariant under a subgroup of rank $d-1$. Extending the Boolean normal-form method of Greenfeld and Tao, we obtain a configuration in the translation orbit closure whose restrictions to cosets of a finite-index lattice are either invariant under a rank-$(d-1)$ subgroup or given by a three-term affine fractional-part formula with density one half. The tiling equations pair the restrictions given by this formula and determine a finite bipartite graph. A two-coloring replaces those restrictions by constants while preserving every tiling equation. We deduce a characterization of periodic tilability by independent companions and a decision algorithm for families containing an independent $(d-1)$-subfamily, with the dimension as part of the input.

</details>


### [74] [Counting Weight-$k$ Italian Dominating Sets on Trees and Cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00108)
*Pingping Shao,Chengye Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究路径、树与环图上意大利支配函数（IDF）按权重的计数问题，将系数计算转化为受约束的整数组合问题，给出闭式表达与O(n²)动态规划算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意大利支配多项式是支配多项式与罗马支配多项式的自然类比，但其系数（各权重的IDF个数）在固定图类上的计算此前缺乏系统研究。作者希望建立统一视角并填补路径、树、环图上精确计数的空白。

Method: 将问题等价转化为"受限整数组合"：引入亏量变量y(v)=2−f(v)，使系数d_I(G,2n−j)等于把j拆成n个不超过2的部分、且满足局部邻接容量约束的组合数。据此推导d_I(C_n,2n−j)（j=0…5）的闭式（三叉系数减去显式枚举的禁形）。算法上分别给出路径、一般树（后序合并+亏量参数）、环图（边界条件化）的O(n²)时间、O(n)空间动态规划，并附正确性证明与复杂度分析。

Result: 得到d_I(C_n,2n−j)（j=0,…,5）的闭式表达；给出路径、树、环图三类图计算D_I全部系数的O(n²)时间/O(n)空间DP算法；所有数值结果经暴力枚举验证，并给出完整表格。

Conclusion: 意大利支配多项式的系数计算本质上是带邻接容量约束的整数组合问题，该视角统一了路径、树、环图上的精确计数并导出高效多项式时间算法；已知的γ_I(P_n)=⌊n/2⌋+1与γ_I(C_n)=⌈n/2⌉以引用方式给出而未重证。

Abstract: We study the problem of counting \emph{Italian dominating functions} (IDFs) of each possible weight on paths, trees, and cycles. An Italian dominating function on a graph $G=(V,E)$ is a function $f\colon V\to\{0,1,2\}$ such that every vertex $v$ with $f(v)=0$ satisfies $\sum_{u\in N(v)}f(u)\ge 2$; the \emph{Italian domination polynomial} $D_I(G,x)=\sum_k d_I(G,k)\,x^k$ records the number $d_I(G,k)$ of such functions of weight~$k$. This polynomial is the Italian analogue of the well-studied domination and Roman domination polynomials, and computing its coefficients on a fixed graph class is, in essence, a \emph{constrained integer composition} problem: passing to the deficit variables $y(v)=2-f(v)$, each coefficient $d_I(G,2n-j)$ counts the number of compositions of $j$ into $n$ parts, each at most~$2$, that additionally satisfy a local adjacency capacity constraint. We make this connection explicit and use it to derive closed-form expressions for $d_I(C_n,2n-j)$, $j=0,\ldots,5$, as trinomial coefficients minus explicitly enumerated forbidden configurations. On the algorithmic side, we give dynamic programming algorithms that compute \emph{all} coefficients of $D_I$ on path graphs ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space), on general trees ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space, via a post-order merge with a deficit parameter), and on cycle graphs ($O(n^2)$ time, $O(n)$ space, via boundary conditioning), together with formal correctness proofs and complexity analyses. The known values $γ_I(P_n)=\lfloor n/2\rfloor+1$ and $γ_I(C_n)=\lceil n/2\rceil$ are recalled with references rather than reproved. All numerical results are verified against brute-force enumeration, and complete tables are provided.

</details>

Result: 确定了 $G(n,r,s)$ 独立数精确稳定性的概率阈值，改进了 Pyaderkin 关于渐近稳定性的既有结果。

Abstract: Let $G(n,r,s)$ be a graph $(V,E)$ such that $V = \binom{[n]}{r}$ and $E = \{(u, v)| |u \cap v| = s\}. $In this paper we study the probability threshold for the exact stability of the independence number of $G(n, r, s)$ where $r > 2s+2$. We improve the previous result of Pyaderkin that implied asymptotic stability of the independence number.

</details>

*Qiyue Tang*

Abstract: For a graph $G=(V,E)$ of order $n$ and an integer $k$ between $1$ and $\lfloor\frac{n}{2}\rfloor$, its token graph $F_k(G)$ is the graph whose vertices consist of the $\binom{n}{k}$ $k$-subsets of $V$, and two vertices of $F_k(G)$ are adjacent whenever their symmetric difference is an edge in $E$. It was found that the algebraic connectivity of a graph is greater than or equal to that of its token graph, while the Laplacian spectral radius of a graph is less than or equal to that of its token graph. Moreover, a conjecture that the algebraic connectivity of a graph coincides with that of its token graph has been proved by using the theory of continuous Markov chains of random walks.
  In this paper, we derive some results about the algebraic connectivities of a graph and the same graph after adding new edges and their token graphs to obtain a combinatorial/algebraic proof. Besides, we provide some conditions under which the Laplacian spectral radius of a graph is less than that of its token graph. Finally, we characterize the graphs that have the same Laplacian spectral radius as their token graphs, including trees.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Jin, Milojević, Tomon and Zhang established a powerful recursive estimate relating the positive eigenvalues of a graph whose least eigenvalue is small in absolute value. We establish a refinement of this result, yielding improved estimates across spectral graph theory and discrepancy theory, and for Chowla's cosine problem. Recent results of Janzer, Tomon and Yip allow us to directly transfer least eigenvalue estimates to the corresponding surplus estimates.
  Using these tools, we fully resolve a conjecture of Räty, Sudakov and Tomon. We show that, for an $n$-vertex graph $G$ with least eigenvalue $λ_n$ and surplus $\operatorname{sp}(G)$, if $G$ is $ε$-far from all disjoint unions of cliques, then $|λ_n|\geq Ω_ε(n^{1/4})$ and $\operatorname{sp}(G)\geq Ω_ε(n^{5/4})$. Furthermore, we show that when $G$ is $n^{-o(1)}$-far from all disjoint unions of cliques, we have $|λ_n|\geq n^{1/4 - o(1)}$ and $\operatorname{sp}(G)\geq n^{5/4 - o(1)}$. Finally, we show that the surplus of a $K_t$-free graph with $m$ edges is at least $m^{0.614 - o(1)}$ as $m$ tends to infinity.

</details>

Conclusion: $A_5$的外幂恒等式为Baez的二项式系数例外巧合提供了统一的对称性解释，其对称性源于二十面体几何结构与稳定子特征标，并在特定维数处（如1540、3003、7140）表现出唯一性或非典范结构。

Abstract: Motivated by John Baez's question about seven exceptional coincidences among binomial coefficients, we construct seven exterior-power identities for representations of $A_5$. Their common symmetry comes from simplex edges and icosahedral vertices, axes, and faces, together with characters of stabilizers. We enumerate all ordered pairs of representation isomorphism classes in the prescribed dimensions. Apart from the pair of trivial actions, the permutation pair at $1540$ and the complex monomial pair at $7140$ are unique. At $3003$, Klein's twelve-point divisor gives a noncanonical intertwiner factored through polynomial division, differentiation, and residues.

</details>


### [81] [Stability conditions and subdivisions of Lawrence polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00591)
*Natasha Crepeau*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文建立了图的稳定性条件、退化集与拟阵单元素扩张之间的对应关系，并通过Lawrence多面体的细分构造稳定性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究节点曲线X及其对偶图G上的稳定性条件。稳定性条件会产生退化集，但退化集也可不依赖稳定性条件来定义。作者希望揭示退化集、稳定性条件与拟阵（及定向拟阵）扩张之间的深层结构联系。

Method: 方法基于拟阵与定向拟阵理论：(1) 将图G的退化集与图形拟阵M(G)的单元素扩张建立对应；(2) 研究哪些单元素扩张可定向为定向图形拟阵M(G)的单元素扩张；(3) 利用定向拟阵单元素扩张与对偶定向拟阵之Lawrence多面体细分之间的双射；(4) 由余图形拟阵M*(G)的Lawrence多面体细分构造G上的稳定性条件；(5) 用退化集偏序中的下界（全部双连通子集）刻画可定向扩张。

Result: 证明了：(1) 图G的退化集与图形拟阵M(G)的单元素扩张一一对应；(2) 部分M(G)单元素扩张可定向为定向图形拟阵M(G)的单元素扩张；(3) 可由余图形拟阵M*(G)的Lawrence多面体细分构造出G上的稳定性条件；(4) 若退化集D在退化集偏序中以G的全部双连通子集为下界，则D对应M(G)的一个可定向扩张。

Conclusion: 退化集是图形拟阵单元素扩张的组合表现，稳定性条件可由Lawrence多面体细分构造，且具有特定下界性质的退化集恰对应可定向扩张，从而将图论、拟阵与多面体几何统一起来。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a nodal curve, and $G$ be the graph dual to $X$. A stability condition on a graph $G$ is an assignment of integers to the biconnected subsets of vertices of $G$ satisfying some desired properties. Stability conditions yield degeneracy sets, which are certain collections of biconnected subsets of $G$, but degeneracy sets can also be described without a reference stability condition. We show that the degeneracy sets of a graph $G$ correspond to the single-element extensions of the graphic matroid $M(G)$. Some single-element extensions of the graphic matroid $M(G)$ can be oriented to be single-element extensions of the oriented graphic matroid $\mathcal{M}(G)$. Single-element extensions of oriented matroids are in bijection with subdivisions of the Lawrence polytope of the dual oriented matroid. We construct stability conditions on $G$ from subdivisions of the Lawrence polytope of the cographic matroid $\mathcal{M}^\ast(G)$. Finally, we show that any degeneracy set $\mathcal{D}$ of a graph $G$ that has the set of all biconnected subsets of $G$ as a lower bound in the poset of degeneracy sets corresponds to an orientable extension of $M(G)$.

</details>


### [82] [A Pick-type theorem for halfway-lattice polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00696)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对该类幺模多边形，其面积可表示为 A = I - V/8 + 1/2，其中I为内部格点数，V为顶点数。

Conclusion: 该公式是Pick定理在"半格边"幺模多边形上的对应形式，扩展了格点多边形面积理论的适用范围。

Abstract: Given a polygon in the plane with vertices on lattice points, Pick's theorem expresses its area A in terms of the number I of lattice points in its interior and the number B of lattice points in its boundary: A = I + B/2 - 1. We consider polygons in the plane whose edges are halfway between lattice points. For such a polygon that is also unimodular, we express its area A in terms of the number I of lattice points in its interior and the number V of its vertices: A = I - V/8 + 1/2 .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Ramsey型思想下，简单1-排列中唯一不可避免的是循环排列：任意足够大的简单1-排列都包含任意固定大小的循环子排列。作者希望将这一不可避免性现象推广到k-排列，探究k增大时不可避免的简单k-排列的种类数量如何变化。

Method: 论文采用组合与Ramsey理论方法研究简单k-排列（任意两条曲线恰交于k点且横穿，无三线共点）。通过构造和分析简单k-排列的结构，证明对于每个m≥3，大小为m的不可避免简单k-排列的数量随k指数增长，且该增长率与m无关；对偶数k，将方法推广到伪圆的k-排列。

Result: 证明了对于每个m≥3，大小为m的不可避免简单k-排列的数量随k呈指数增长，并且这一增长与m无关。对于偶数k，得到伪圆k-排列的类似结论。

Conclusion: k-排列的不可避免简单结构远比1-排列丰富：随k增大，不可避免的简单k-排列种类指数增长，且与目标子排列大小m无关；该现象对偶数k的伪圆排列同样成立。

Abstract: A $k$-arrangement of pseudolines is a set of bi-infinite curves in the plane such that any two of them intersect each other in exactly $k$ points, at which they cross, and it is simple if no three curves meet at a common point. Cyclic arrangements are the only simple $1$-arrangements of pseudolines that are unavoidable, in the Ramsey spirit: for each fixed $m\ge 1$, every sufficiently large simple $1$-arrangement of pseudolines has a cyclic subarrangement of size $m$. We show that, for every $m\ge 3$, the number of unavoidable simple $k$-arrangements of pseudolines of size $m$ grows exponentially with $k$, independently of $m$. For even $k$, we prove an analogous result for $k$-arrangements of pseudocircles.

</details>

Method: 以 OEIS A005773 为 Motzkin-前缀序列构造两个 Riordan 数组，利用其生成矩阵（production matrices）之间的边界分解 (boundary factorization) 建立联系，导出周期三变换；同时对偏移 Hankel 矩阵给出统一分解，用于计算高阶 Hankel 行列式。

Conclusion: 生成矩阵的边界分解是刻画 Riordan 数组间联系的有效工具，可统一处理端点分布的模周期现象与高阶 Hankel 行列式的显式计算，并可望推广到更高阶情形。

Abstract: We study two Riordan arrays associated with the Motzkin-prefix sequence (\href{https://oeis.org/A005773}{OEIS A005773}). By connecting their production matrices through a simple boundary factorization, we obtain a period-three transformation on Motzkin endpoint distributions and exact formulas for paths by final height modulo~$3$. In addition, a unified factorization for the shifted Hankel matrices leads directly to explicit closed forms for the third and fourth Hankel determinants, $Δ_n^{(3)}$ and~$Δ_n^{(4)}$.

</details>

Abstract: Let $K_t$ be obtained by attaching a pendant path of length $t$ to a fixed rooted graph $K$. We prove that $\mathrm{Mo}(L(K_t))-\mathrm{Mo}(K_t)$, the difference between its line-graph and original Mostar indices, is exactly affine on each parity class beyond a sharp uniform cutoff. The slope depends on distance-level neighbor counts and is positive for every non-bipartite core. Triangle chains give infinitely many graphs with $\mathrm{Mo}(L(G))>\mathrm{Mo}(G)$ at every positive cyclomatic number $c$, with maximum degree three, resolving Alex-Indulal Problem 3.3. At zero slope, a cactus branch-mass formula decides equality; identical vertex profiles need not give identical intercepts. Fixed cores giving equality for all sufficiently long attachments exist exactly when $c$ is odd. Maximum degree three suffices, and at every even $c$ a binary-tree construction gives equality on one eventual parity class.
