<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brandt猜想每个n阶正则无三角形图的最小无符号拉普拉斯特征值至多为4n/25；Balogh等人用旗代数将无三角形图的上界加强到15n/94。本文进一步考察额外排除五边形的效果，探究对最小无符号拉普拉斯特征值界的影响。

Method: 结合七顶点旗代数不等式与局部Rayleigh约束，在计数论证过程中始终保留最小特征值；并使用精确整数证书验证所需不等式。方法不依赖任何正则性假设。

Result: 证明了每个n阶无{C3,C5}图G满足qmin(G)<0.0569n，且无需正则性假设。

Conclusion: 额外排除五边形可进一步降低最小无符号拉普拉斯特征值的上界，表明无{C3,C5}结构对无符号拉普拉斯最小特征值有更强的限制。

Abstract: Brandt [Discrete Math. 183 (1998) 17--25] conjectured that the least signless Laplacian eigenvalue of every regular triangle-free graph of order $n$ is at most $4n/25$. Using flag algebras, Balogh, Clemen, Lidický, Norin and Volec [SIAM J. Discrete Math. 37 (2023) 1173--1179] established the stronger bound $15n/94$ for all triangle-free graphs. We investigate the effect of additionally excluding pentagons and prove that every $\{C_3,C_5\}$-free graph $G$ of order $n$ satisfies $\qmin(G)<0.0569n$, without any regularity assumption. The proof combines seven-vertex flag inequalities with local Rayleigh constraints that retain the least eigenvalue throughout the counting argument. An exact integer certificate establishes the required inequality.

</details>


### [88] [A Hadamard Cube of Order 22](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01065)
*Amin Bahmanian,Sho Suda*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文显式构造了一个 $22$ 阶三维 Hadamard 矩阵，并通过列出其 22 个 $22\times22$ 二维切片予以展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维 Hadamard 矩阵是一类具有特殊正交性质的组合结构，其存在性与显式构造长期受到关注。此类构造通常难以获得，因此给出一个具体实例具有记录与验证价值。

Method: 采用显式构造法：直接给出一个三维 Hadamard 矩阵，以 $22$ 个 $22\times22$ 二维切片的列表形式完整呈现该矩阵。

Result: 成功给出了一个 $22$ 阶三维 Hadamard 矩阵的显式实例。

Conclusion: 该 $22$ 阶三维 Hadamard 矩阵的存在性得到确证，其切片列表形式便于进一步检验与应用。

Abstract: We give an explicit three-dimensional Hadamard matrix of order $22$. The example is presented by listing its twenty-two $22\times22$ two-dimensional slices.

</details>


### [89] [Descent Tops and Pinnacles on 2143- and 3421-Avoiding Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01077)
*Yue Dong,Lily Li Liu,Tongyuan Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明Burstein猜想中2143-3421情形成立，通过构造显式双射，并由此证明Dumont第一类排列上2143与3421的Wilf等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burstein猜想涉及descent-top集合在回避类上的分布，其2143-3421情形此前未被解决；同时Burstein与Jones关于Dumont第一类排列上2143与3421 Wilf等价性的猜想也待证明。

Method: 结合最大Cartesian树上的一个对合与极大递减段末元素的交换操作，在两个回避类之间构造显式双射；在中间回避类上证明交换过程终止且输出与选择无关，从而给出显式逆映射。

Result: 构造出保持pinnacle集、并反转pinnacle值左右顺序的双射；将该双射限制到Dumont第一类排列上，得到2143与3421的Wilf等价性。

Conclusion: 解决了Burstein猜想的2143-3421情形，并证明了Burstein-Jones猜想。

Abstract: We prove the 2143-3421 case of Burstein's conjecture on the distribution of descent-top sets by constructing an explicit bijection between the two avoidance classes. The bijection also preserves the pinnacle set and reverses the left-to-right order of the pinnacle values. We combine an involution on maximum Cartesian trees with exchanges of the final entries of maximal decreasing runs. On an intermediate avoidance class, we prove that the exchange procedures terminate and that the output in each direction is independent of the choices made. This gives an explicit inverse. Restricting the bijection to Dumont permutations of the first kind proves the conjecture of Burstein and Jones that 2143 and 3421 are Wilf-equivalent on this class

</details>


### [90] [Upper Bounds on the Turán Density of Hypergraphs Associated with the Projective Plane over a Finite Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01084)
*Subhankar Dash,Kaushik Majumder*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 利用 L^p 方法与射影平面关联结构，给出 t-页书 B_t[PG(2,q)] 的 Turán 密度上界 (q(q+1)^{-1})^{1/(qt)}，并由此改进 PG(2,q) 的已知上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PG(2,q) 的 Turán 密度上界先前为 1 - binom(q^2, q)^{-1}，该界较粗糙，需要更精确的估计；同时对由 PG(2,q) 构成的 t-页书 B_t[PG(2,q)] 的 Turán 密度缺乏有效上界。

Method: 采用 L^p-方法（L^p-method），并结合有限射影平面 PG(2,q) 的关联结构（incidence structure）进行分析。

Result: 对任意正整数 t，证明 B_t[PG(2,q)] 的 Turán 密度上界为 (q(q+1)^{-1})^{1/(qt)}；取 t=1 时，将 PG(2,q) 的 Turán 密度上界由 1 - binom(q^2, q)^{-1} 改进为 (q(q+1)^{-1})^{1/q}。

Conclusion: L^p-方法与射影平面关联结构的结合有效给出了 t-页书的 Turán 密度上界，并显著改进了 (q+1)-图 PG(2,q) 的既有上界。

Abstract: Let $\PG(2,q)$ denote the projective plane over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$ where $q\geq2$ is a prime power. For a positive integer $t$, let $B_{t}[\PG(2,q)]$ denote the $t$-page book obtained from $t-$many copies of the $(q+1)-$graph $\PG(2,q)$, sharing a common edge. Employing the $\mathsf{L}^p-$method and using the incidence structure of the projective plane, we establish that the upper bound of Turán density of $B_{t}[\PG(2,q)]$ is $(q(q+1)^{-1})^{\frac{1}{qt}}$. For the $(q+1)-$graph $\PG(2,q)$, the previously known upper bound on its Turán density was $1-\binom{q^2}{q}^{-1}$. As a consequence of our estimate, this upper bound is improved to $(q(q+1)^{-1})^{\frac{1}{q}}$.

</details>


### [91] [Perfect matching covers of claw-free cubic graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01137)
*Huaibin Zhou,Xiumei Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Berge猜想在无桥无爪三次图上成立，并给出四个完美匹配覆盖边数比例的下界299/315。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berge猜想与5-圈双覆盖猜想是图论中关于覆盖问题的核心猜想。Hakobyan和Mkrtchyan已证明5-圈双覆盖猜想等价于无桥无爪三次图可被四个完美匹配覆盖，因此研究无桥无爪三次图上的覆盖问题是推进相关猜想的重要切入点。

Method: 针对无桥无爪三次图，通过分析其结构与匹配性质，证明其可被五个完美匹配覆盖，并对四个完美匹配所能覆盖的边数进行定量估计。

Result: 证明了Berge猜想对所有无桥无爪三次图成立；同时证明每个无桥无爪三次图G存在四个完美匹配，其覆盖边数至少为(299/315)|E(G)|。

Conclusion: 本文推进了Berge猜想与5-圈双覆盖猜想在无爪三次图类上的研究，给出了四个完美匹配覆盖比例的非平凡下界，为解决更一般的覆盖猜想提供了支持。

Abstract: The Berge conjecture [Proc. London Math. Soc., 1979] asserts that every bridgeless cubic graph can be covered by five perfect matchings. The 5-cycle double cover conjecture, proposed independently by Preissmann (1981) and Celmins (1984), asserts that every bridgeless graph admits five even subgraphs such that every edge belongs to exactly two of them. Hakobyan and Mkrtchyan [Ars Math. Contemp., 2019] proved that the 5-cycle double cover conjecture holds if and only if every bridgeless claw-free cubic graph can be covered by four perfect matchings. In this paper, we prove that the Berge conjecture holds for bridgeless claw-free cubic graphs. Moreover, we show that every bridgeless claw-free cubic graph $G$ admits four perfect matchings that cover at least $\frac{299}{315}|E(G)|$ edges.

</details>


### [92] [Intersecting integer partitions: star bounds and counterexamples at every scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01747)
*Yury Person,Thomas Schweser*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究了将 $n$ 分拆为恰好 $k$ 个正整数部分的 $t$-相交族最大规模问题：证明在 $n\ge Ak^3$ 时规范 $t$-星最大，并给出对所有相交尺度下 Borg 猜想的反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整数分拆的相交族极值问题是组合极值理论的自然对象。此前缺乏对固定部分数 $k$ 时最大 $t$-相交族在大 $n$ 情形的统一刻画，且 Borg 猜想是否成立尚不明确，需要确定阈值并获得反例。

Method: 以"至少含 $t$ 个 1"的分拆所构成的规范 $t$-星为候选极值族；应用 Kupavskii 的弱散布（weak-spread）定理进行上界估计，得到 $n\ge Ak^3$（任意固定 $A>24$、$k$ 充分大）时规范星最大，且对 $1\le t<k$ 一致成立。

Result: 证明当 $n\ge Ak^3$（$A>24$ 固定，$k$ 足够大）时规范 $t$-星对所有 $1\le t<k$ 都是最大 $t$-相交族；同时构造反例：对充分大 $k$ 及任意 $1\le d<k$，可取 $d/4\le t\le d$ 且 $n=\lfloor tk^2/3\rfloor$，使某 $t$-相交族严格大于规范星，从而在每个相交尺度下推翻 Borg 猜想。

Conclusion: 结果统一刻画了 $n$ 远大于 $k^3$ 时最大 $t$-相交分拆族，即规范 $t$-星；但在 $n\sim tk^2$ 的中等区间，Borg 猜想普遍失效，说明该阈值在数量级上是必要的。

Abstract: Two integer partitions $t$-intersect if they have at least $t$ common parts, counted with multiplicity. We study the largest $t$-intersecting families of integer partitions of $n$ into exactly $k$ positive parts. The canonical $t$-star consists of the partitions containing at least $t$ ones. Applying Kupavskii's weak-spread theorem, we prove that this star is largest whenever $n\ge Ak^3$, for every fixed $A>24$ and all sufficiently large $k$, uniformly over $1\le t<k$. We also give counterexamples to Borg's conjecture at every intersection scale: for all sufficiently large $k$ and for every $1\le d<k$, one may choose $d/4\le t\le d$ and $n=\lfloor tk^2/3\rfloor$ so that a $t$-intersecting family is strictly larger than the canonical star.

</details>


### [93] [A Proof of the Third Borwein Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01156)
*Yicen Ma*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究多项式 $S_n(q)=\prod_{j=1}^n\prod_{s=1}^4(1-q^{5j-s})$ 的系数符号，证明第三 Borwein 猜想所预测的符号模式对所有正整数 $n$ 成立：当 $n\ge1750$ 时由四峰分析给出解析证明，$1\le n\le1749$ 由有限整数验证补齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证第三 Borwein 猜想所预测的系数符号模式。该猜想断言 $S_n(q)$ 的系数符号呈周期性/稀疏的确定模式，此前缺乏对一般 $n$ 的严格证明。核心困难在于：需要一种能同时控制实鞍点附近振幅、并精确处理模 $5$ 剩余类 $3$、$4$ 中出现的抵消现象的解析方法，且需给出显式常数以衔接大规模解析估计与小范围有限检验。

Method: 1) 对 $S_n(q)$ 系数进行"四峰分析"（four-peak analysis）：识别鞍点附近的四个主要贡献峰及它们之间的干涉。2) 将剩余类 $3$ 和 $4$ 中的关键抵消保留为组合振幅中的精确因子 $e^{-5z}$，从而得到平移后的鞍点方程 $d-5n=n^2\beta(t)$。3) 采用两层分拆单射（two-layer partition injection）给出所需的线性边界，把解析估计区间与小次数区间衔接。4) 所有连续参数估计均给出显式常数，其有限算术比较以有理证书形式提供。5) 辅以精确整数验证方案。

Result: 对每个 $n\ge1750$ 及每个系数，证明了第三 Borwein 猜想预测的符号模式。结合作者报告的 $1\le n\le1749$ 有限验证的完成，得到该符号模式对每个正整数 $n$ 成立。附带的系数记录覆盖 $1\le n\le500$；报告的全范围计算被单独标注。

Conclusion: 第三 Borwein 猜想所预测的符号模式对所有正整数 $n$ 成立。证明策略为"大 $n$ 解析 + 小 $n$ 有限验证"的混合方法：解析部分通过四峰分析与精确抵消因子 $e^{-5z}$ 导出平移鞍点方程，并以显式常数和有理证书保证有限算术比较的可靠性；两层分拆单射提供衔接边界的线性界。附带的系数记录仅覆盖 $n\le500$，而全范围计算被明确区分标注。

Abstract: We study the coefficients of $S_n(q)=\prod_{j=1}^n\prod_{s=1}^4(1-q^{5j-s})$. An effective four-peak analysis gives the sign pattern predicted by the third Borwein conjecture for every $n\ge1750$ and every coefficient. The essential cancellation in residue classes $3$ and $4$ is retained as an exact factor $e^{-5z}$ in the combined amplitude, leading to the shifted saddle point equation $d-5n=n^2β(t)$. A two-layer partition injection provides the linear boundary needed to join this analysis to small degrees. All continuous parameter estimates have explicit constants; their finite arithmetic comparisons are supplied as rational certificates. We also describe exact integer verification. Combining the analytic theorem with the author's reported completion of the finite verification for $1\le n\le1749$ gives the conjectured sign pattern for every positive integer $n$. The available supplementary coefficient record covers $1\le n\le500$; the reported full-range computation is identified separately.

</details>


### [94] [Recurrent two-dimensional sequences over three and four letters simulating von Neumann constructions of finite ordinals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01753)
*Xiaofang Jiang,Mihai Prunescu,Bogdan Dumitru*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究三类递归双序列（其着色图编码有限序数构造的镜像版本），显式描述单元格、刻画矩形嵌套序，并对四字母情形区分普通盒与虚拟盒、证明装配规则；最后给出嵌套盒数公式并证明这些序列均非k-自动序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限序数构造（如von Neumann构造）在递归双序列与着色图上的镜像实现，刻画其结构规律与嵌套几何，并探讨其可计算/自动序列性质。

Method: 研究对象为三条递归双序列：两条三字母序列分别实现具有两个和三个初始符号的变体，一条四字母序列由单个初始符号实现von Neumann构造。对每条序列显式描述单元格并识别矩形的有序嵌套；四字母情形进一步区分普通盒与虚拟盒并证明其装配规则。证明沿反对角线用归纳法进行；对一初始符号与三初始符号的构造，将递推归约为有限局部构型集合并用计算机验证。推导嵌套盒数公式（含算术项表示），并证明三条序列均非k-自动（k≥2）。

Result: 给出了三条序列的单元格显式描述与矩形嵌套序；证明了四字母情形普通盒与虚拟盒的装配规则；得到嵌套盒数公式及其算术项表示；证明三条序列对任意整数k≥2均非k-自动序列。

Conclusion: 该工作以递归双序列为框架统一刻画有限序数构造的镜像变体，结合归纳证明与计算机验证给出结构与计数结果，并通过非k-自动性结果揭示这些序列在自动序列意义下的复杂性。

Abstract: We study three recurrent double sequences whose colored diagrams encode mirrored versions of the finite-ordinal construction. Two sequences use three letters and realize variants with two and three initial symbols. A four-letter sequence realizes the von Neumann construction from one initial symbol. For each sequence we describe its cells explicitly and identify the ordered nesting of its rectangles. For four letters, we distinguish ordinary and virtual boxes and prove their assembly rules. The proofs proceed by induction along antidiagonals. For the constructions with one and three initial symbols, we reduce the recurrence to finite sets of local configurations and verify those sets by computer. We derive formulas for the numbers of nested boxes, including arithmetic-term representations, and we prove that none of the three sequences is $k$-automatic for any integer $k \geq 2$.

</details>


### [95] [A proof of Lehmer's permutation conjecture for neighbor-swap graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01240)
*Tom Verhoeff*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Lehmer关于多重集排列在相邻交换下存在"不完美哈密顿遍历"的猜想，通过Verhoeff(2017)的非口吃词图N(S)哈密顿性重构，结合超立方体划分、Stachowiak定理以及两个显式循环(28与84词)，完成了证明并用Python与Lean 4验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lehmer(1965)提出多重集排列的相邻交换遍历猜想，被Knuth列为未解研究问题；Verhoeff(2017)将其重构为图N(S)的哈密顿性问题，并识别出两个例外族。本文旨在彻底证明该重构猜想，从而解决Lehmer猜想。

Method: 将词按相同多米诺内容划分为类，类内交换构成超立方体，口吃词恰为0维类；对全偶数重数情形，沿生成树粘合各超立方体的哈密顿环；恰有一个奇重数情形归约到全偶数情形及Stachowiak(1992)定理；两个及以上奇重数由该定理一并解决。仅剩两个显式循环(28词与84词)作为有限检查。

Result: 证明了Verhoeff重构后的猜想，进而证明Lehmer猜想；例外族为奇重数二进制签名与(2k,1,1)排列，它们只有哈密顿路径而无环；所有构造经Python实现并与暴力图校验，证明在Mathlib上的Lean 4中形式化。

Conclusion: Lehmer猜想及其Verhoeff重构形式均获证明，方法兼具结构与计算验证，并完成机器形式化。

Abstract: In 1965, D. H. Lehmer conjectured that the permutations of every multiset admit an imperfect Hamiltonian traversal by adjacent swaps: a walk in the neighbor-swap graph that visits every word, with some words visited twice in order to reach a neighbor and return. The question is posed as an unsolved research problem in Knuth's Art of Computer Programming. Verhoeff (2017) chose the stutter words, in which every domino is a double, as the words to be reached this way, and reformulated the conjecture as the Hamiltonicity of the graph $N(S)$ on the non-stutter words, with two exceptional families --- binary signatures with an odd multiplicity, and the permutations of $(2k,1,1)$ --- that admit a Hamiltonian path but no cycle. This article proves the reformulated conjecture, and with it Lehmer's conjecture. The key structure is a partition of the words into hypercubes: the swaps inside dominoes turn each class of words with the same domino contents into a hypercube, and the stutters are exactly the $0$-dimensional classes. When every multiplicity is even, Hamiltonian cycles of the hypercubes are glued along a spanning tree, with no finite check. The case of exactly one odd multiplicity reduces to the all-even case and to a theorem of Stachowiak (1992), the one inherited Hamiltonicity input, which also settles two or more odd multiplicities. The only finite ingredients are two explicit cycles, of 28 and 84 words. Every construction is implemented in Python and checked against brute-force graphs, and the proof is formalized in Lean 4 over Mathlib.

</details>


### [96] [On $1$-limited and $(1,2)$-domination in cubic graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01796)
*Goran Radić,Aleksandra Tepeh*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明 1-Limited Dominating Set 在 2-连通平面三次图上 NP-完全，并确定 Goldberg 族的 1-limited 支配数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补齐所有固定正整数 k 的 k-Limited Dominating Set 复杂度结果，并为 (1,2)-支配及导出圈相关问题提供新的三次图族证据。

Method: 通过构造/归约证明 1-Limited Dominating Set 在 2-连通平面三次图上的 NP-完全性；对 Goldberg 族（三次图无穷族）计算精确的 1-limited 支配数。

Result: 1-Limited Dominating Set 在 2-连通平面三次图上仍为 NP-完全；完成 k-Limited Dominating Set 对所有固定正整数 k 的复杂度刻画；给出 Goldberg 族的精确 1-limited 支配数。

Conclusion: 该结果闭合 k-Limited Dominating Set 的复杂度谱系，并提供支持 (1,2)-支配与导出圈若干猜想/界的无穷三次图族。

Abstract: A dominating set $D$ of a graph is called $1$-limited if every vertex of $D$ has at most one neighbor outside $D$, while a $(1,2)$-dominating set is a dominating set in which every vertex of the set has at least two neighbors within the set. These two notions coincide on cubic graphs. We prove that the decision problem 1-Limited Dominating Set is $\mathsf{NP}$-complete even when restricted to $2$-connected planar cubic graphs, thereby completing the known complexity results for $k$-Limited Dominating Set for all fixed positive integers $k$. We also determine the exact $1$-limited domination number of the entire Goldberg family. This provides a further infinite family of cubic graphs supporting several open conjectures and proposed bounds concerning $(1,2)$-domination and induced cycles.

</details>


### [97] [Matroid liftability and Oxley--Wang derived matroids, with connections to scene analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01854)
*Sean Dewar,Emiliano Liwski,Signe Lundqvist,Fatemeh Mohammadi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将 liftability 概念从 paving matroids 推广到具有可实现截断的 matroids，引入 generic realisability，并用 Oxley–Wang derived matroids 建立统一框架进行刻画与研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liftability 最初仅为 paving matroids 引入，用于系统构造 matroid variety 理想中的多项式，适用范围有限；作者希望将其推广到具有 realisable truncations 的更一般 matroids，并寻找统一的刻画工具。

Method: 将 liftability 推广至具有 realisable truncations 的 matroids；引入 generic realisability；研究二者在固定截断的 matroid 格中弱序下的行为；借助 generic matroid realisations 的 Oxley–Wang derived matroid 建立统一框架，用其刻画 liftability 与 generic realisability；通过 matroid realisation space 的坐标截面对 generic Oxley–Wang derived matroids 的基作代数描述，并与 slack realisation spaces 建立联系。

Result: 给出了 liftability 与 generic realisability 的 Oxley–Wang derived matroid 刻画；获得 liftable 与 generically realisable paving matroids 的组合刻画，并与 Whiteley 的 scene analysis 理论相联系，通过 Oxley–Wang derived matroids 给出 Whiteley 关于超图允许非平凡（sharp）scenes 刻画的新证明；计算了一类稀疏 paving matroids 的 generic Oxley–Wang derived matroid。

Conclusion: 该工作把 liftability 与 generic realisability 纳入统一的 Oxley–Wang derived matroid 框架，连接 matroid 理论、代数几何与 scene analysis，并为相关组合刻画提供新证明与计算实例。

Abstract: Liftability was first introduced for paving matroids, where it was used to systematically construct polynomials in the ideals of the associated matroid varieties. In this paper, we generalise the notion of liftability to matroids with realisable truncations. We additionally introduce the notion of generic realisability of a matroid; a strong notion of realisability that implies both realisability and liftability of the matroid. We study the behaviour of liftability and generic realisability under the weak order of matroids within the lattice of matroids with fixed truncation. We develop a unified framework for studying liftability of matroids through Oxley--Wang derived matroid of generic matroid realisations. We characterise liftability and generic realisability in terms of Oxley--Wang derived matroids. Moreover, we give an algebraic description of the bases of the generic Oxley--Wang derived matroids in terms of coordinate sections of matroid realisation spaces and relate our constructions to slack realisation spaces of matroids.
  As an application of our framework, we obtain a combinatorial characterisation of liftable and generically realisable paving matroids and connect it with Whiteley's theory of scene analysis. In particular, our approach gives a new proof, via Oxley--Wang derived matroids, of Whiteley's characterisation of hypergraphs admitting non-trivial (sharp) scenes. Finally, we compute the generic Oxley--Wang derived matroid for a special class of sparse paving matroids.

</details>


### [98] [An explicit basis for the extended Catalan arrangement of type Dl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01866)
*Weili Guo,Yupeng Wang,Zixuan Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文借助 Kawanoue 的有限和公式构造，显式给出 Dl 型扩展 Catalan 排列锥的导子模的齐次基，并证明该基在无穷远处的限制与 Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga 的整基一致（相差非零常数），从而揭示了该整基参数选择背后的几何动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga 构造的整基中参数选择缺乏几何解释，本文试图为其找到几何动机。

Method: 受 Kawanoue 基于有限和公式的构造启发，显式构造 Dl 型扩展 Catalan 排列锥的导子模的齐次基。

Result: 所构造基在无穷远处的限制与 Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga 的整基相差一个非零常数，二者本质一致。

Conclusion: 该一致性刻画了 Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga 整基参数选择背后的几何来源。

Abstract: Motivated by Kawanoue's construction with the finite-sum formulas, we construct an explicit homogeneous basis for the derivation module of the cone of the extended Catalan arrangement of type Dl. We further show that the restriction of this basis at infinity coincides, up to a nonzero constant, with the integral basis constructed by Feigin-Wang-Yoshinaga. This identifies the geometric motivation behind the parameter choices.

</details>


### [99] [Balanced weightings and permutation matchings applied to the monoid of order-preserving mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01912)
*Peter M. Higgins*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出有限半群组合D-类具有排列匹配的充要条件：其结构矩阵存在平衡加权（行和、列和分别恒定的非负权），以此弱化正则性假设，并用Birkhoff–von Neumann定理替代Hall引理；进而证明n元链上所有保序自映射构成的幺半群具有排列匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果通常依赖D-类的正则性假设，并借助Hall引理来保证排列匹配的存在。作者希望找到更一般的判定准则，放宽正则性这一较强条件，同时用更合适的矩阵工具替换Hall引理。

Method: 将D-类的结构矩阵作为研究对象，引入"平衡加权"概念（非负权且行和、列和分别恒定）；证明排列匹配的存在性等价于该矩阵存在平衡加权，证明中利用Birkhoff–von Neumann定理（双随机矩阵作为置换矩阵的凸组合）取代Hall引理；随后将该准则应用于n元链的保序自映射幺半群。

Result: 得到D-类具有排列匹配的充要条件：结构矩阵可平衡加权，从而弱化了正则性假设；并据此证明n元链上所有保序自映射构成的幺半群存在排列匹配。

Conclusion: 排列匹配的存在性可完全由结构矩阵的平衡加权刻画，正则性并非必要；Birkhoff–von Neumann定理在此类问题中是Hall引理的有效替代工具，该框架可推广到保序变换幺半群等具体情形。

Abstract: We give a necessary and sufficient condition for a combinatorial D-class of a finite semigroup to have a permutation matching: that its structure matrix admit a balanced weighting, that is, non-negative weights with constant row sums and constant column sums. This weakens the regularity hypothesis, the role of Hall's lemma being taken up by the Birkhoff--von Neumann theorem. We then apply it to show that the monoid of all order-preserving self-maps of an n-element chain, has a permutation matching.

</details>


### [100] [Improved upper bounds on the list chromatic number of $K_t$-minor-free graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01946)
*Yangyan Gu,Rongxing Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明每个 \(K_t\)-minor-free 图的选择数（choice number）为 \(O(t\log\log t)\)，并给出 \(n\) 顶点情形下的强界 \(O(t\log(2+n/t))\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究是否所有 \(K_t\)-minor-free 图都是 \(O(t)\)-choosable 这一开放问题，缩小该图类选择数上界与线性猜想之间的差距。

Method: 首先证明 \(n\) 顶点 \(K_t\)-minor-free 图的选择数为 \(O(t\log(2+n/t))\)；再以此界作为关键工具，沿用 Delcourt 与 Postle 提出的方法，证明一般 \(K_t\)-minor-free 图的选择数为 \(O(t\log\log t)\)。

Result: 得到两个选择数上界：\(n\) 顶点情形为 \(O(t\log(2+n/t))\)；一般情形为 \(O(t\log\log t)\)。后者改进了 Postle 的 \(O(t(\log\log t)^6)\) 以及 Delcourt–Postle 备注中的 \(O(t(\log\log t)^2)\)。

Conclusion: \(K_t\)-minor-free 图类具有 \(O(t\log\log t)\) 的选择数，显著逼近 \(O(t)\)-choosable 的猜想。

Abstract: It remains open whether every $K_t$-minor-free graph is $O(t)$-choosable. Postle proved that every $K_t$-minor-free graph has choice number $O(t(\log\log t)^6)$. At the end of an earlier version of a paper establishing an $O(t\log\log t)$ bound on the chromatic number of $K_t$-minor-free graphs, Delcourt and Postle remarked that their methods, combined with Postle's earlier techniques, yield an $O(t(\log\log t)^2)$ bound on the choice number. In this paper, we first prove that every $n$-vertex $K_t$-minor-free graph has choice number $O(t\log(2+n/t))$. Using this bound as a key ingredient, we follow the approach outlined by Delcourt and Postle to prove that every $K_t$-minor-free graph is $O(t\log\log t)$-choosable.

</details>


### [101] [Matchings and shape-Wilf-Equivalence of sets of patterns of length three II: Quadruples and Quintuples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01996)
*Sucharita Biswas,Umesh Shankar,Sivaramakrishnan Sivasubramanian*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在长度3的triple模式shape-Wilf等价分类基础上，完成四元组（quadruples）和五元组（quintuples）的分类，并为若干类给出避免横截的显式公式及匹配计数的双射解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长度3的三元模式shape-Wilf等价分类已完成，但更大的模式集（四元组、五元组）具有三元情况编码方法无法捕捉的结构特征，需要新的组合工具来补全分类。

Method: 核心新工具是含阶梯板的Ferrers板上的Dyck路径方法：在关联Dyck路径上引入distinguished cells及相关结构参数，从而获得新的shape-Wilf等价性并推导固定Ferrers板上避免横截数的显式公式；再借助Bloom–Elizalde的模式保持双射研究对应的避免模式完美匹配。

Result: 完成了四元组与五元组的shape-Wilf等价分类；获得若干类新的shape-Wilf等价及固定Ferrers板上避免横截的显式公式；导出匹配计数的递推关系，并用grand Dyck路径、Schröder路径和Dyck路径给出若干匹配枚举序列的双射解释。

Conclusion: 与关于triple的姊妹论文一起，本文完成了长度3模式子集的shape-Wilf等价类分类（除空集和全集这两个平凡情形外），且所构造的双射提供了分类之外的额外组合结构。

Abstract: Building on our classification of shape-Wilf-equivalence classes for triples of patterns of length three, we complete the classification for quadruples and quintuples. The larger pattern sets exhibit structural features that are not captured by the encoding methods used for triples and require additional combinatorial tools. Our main new ingredient is a Dyck-path approach to Ferrers boards containing the staircase board. By introducing distinguished cells and related structural parameters on the associated Dyck paths, we obtain new shape-Wilf-equivalences and, for several classes, explicit formulas for the number of avoiding transversals on a fixed Ferrers board. We further study the corresponding pattern-avoiding perfect matchings through the pattern-preserving bijection of Bloom and Elizalde. Besides deriving recurrence relations, we give bijective interpretations of several matching enumeration sequences in terms of grand Dyck paths, Schröder paths, and Dyck paths. These constructions provide additional combinatorial structure beyond the classification itself. Together with the companion paper on triples, the present work completes the classification of shape-Wilf-equivalence classes for subsets of patterns of length three, apart from the trivial empty and full pattern sets.

</details>


### [102] [Linear circumference in vertex-transitive graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02053)
*Jie Ma,Ziyuan Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了连通顶点传递图中存在长度至少为 cn 的圈（c 为绝对常数），并对大度数情形得到 (1-d^{-1/100})n 的线性下界，首次向 Lovász 哈密顿性猜想给出线性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lovász 提出连通顶点传递图是否必含哈密顿圈的猜想，此前缺乏线性长度的下界结果；本文旨在取得首个线性界进展。

Method: 结合 DeVos 与 Mohar 关于顶点传递图的结构性结果，以及作者提出的用于寻找长圈的一般性框架。

Result: 任意 n≥3 个顶点的连通顶点传递图含长度至少 cn 的圈（c>0 为绝对常数）；当度 d 足够大时，含长度至少 (1-d^{-1/100})n 的圈。

Conclusion: 首次为 Lovász 哈密顿性猜想建立线性界，且所提出的长圈框架可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: We prove that there is an absolute constant $c>0$ such that every connected vertex-transitive graph $G$ on $n \ge 3$ vertices contains a cycle of length at least $cn$. Moreover, every such graph with sufficiently large degree $d$ contains a cycle of length at least $(1-d^{-1/100})n$. This gives the first linear bound towards Lovász's Hamiltonicity conjecture. The proof combines a structural result of DeVos and Mohar on vertex-transitive graphs with a general framework for finding long cycles, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [103] [Linear arboricity conjecture for infinite graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02065)
*Leandro Aurichi,Rodrigo Santos Monteiro,Caio Fernando Rodrigues*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文将线性荫度猜想推广到有限最大度的无限图，证明其有限与无限版本等价，引入拓扑线性荫度概念并给出与线性荫度的差异界及特定正则图上的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性荫度猜想是图论中关于边分解的经典问题，但以往主要针对有限图。作者希望把该猜想拓展到无限图，并探索线性森林中禁止拓扑圆（即不含圈的无限结构）这一拓扑限制对分解数的影响。

Method: 定义线性荫度$\la(G)$为将边集分解为最大度不超过2的森林所需的最少数量；将线性荫度猜想推广至有限最大度的无限图；引入拓扑线性荫度$\latop(G)$，要求分解中每个线性森林不含拓扑圆；通过构造性证明论证有限与无限版本的等价性，并比较$\latop$与$\la$的差距。

Result: 证明了线性荫度猜想的有限版本与无限版本等价；证明拓扑线性荫度与线性荫度之差至多为1；证明任意围长至少为$2k$的$2k$-正则图的拓扑线性荫度至多为$k+1$。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了线性荫度猜想在有限与无限图上的表述，并通过拓扑线性荫度的引入给出了新的分解上界，深化了对线性森林边分解结构的理解。

Abstract: The linear arboricity $\la(G)$ of a graph $G$ is the least cardinality of linear forests, that is, forests of maximum degree at most $2$, into which its edge set $E(G)$ can be decomposed. The Linear Arboricity Conjecture asserts that $\la(G)\leq\lceil(Δ(G)+1)/2\rceil$ for every finite graph $G$, where $Δ(G)$ denotes the maximum degree of $G$. We extend this conjecture to infinite graphs of finite maximum degree and prove that its finite and infinite versions are equivalent. We introduce topological linear arboricity $\latop (G)$ by requiring the linear forests to contain no topological circle, and show that it differs from linear arboricity by at most one. Finally, we prove that every $2k$-regular graph of girth at least $2k$ has topological linear arboricity at most $k+1$.

</details>


### [104] [Typical intersecting families at $n=2k+1$ and $n=2k+2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02119)
*Lina Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 确定 $2k+1$ 与 $2k+2$ 元集合上 $k$-一致相交族的典型结构，给出计数渐近公式并验证 Balogh–Garcia–Li–Wagner 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相交族极值问题中，最大相交族由 Erdős–Ko–Rado 定理刻画，但"典型"（随机）相交族的结构与数量此前未知，尤其 $n=2k+1,2k+2$ 的临界情形存在猜想待证。

Method: 结合 Sapozhenko 图容器方法控制远离任意星形的族，利用 Kneser 图中的稳定性分析；对靠近固定星形的族采用 polymer 模型与簇展开进行计数枚举。

Result: $n=2k+2$ 时几乎所有相交族平凡，数量为 $(2k+2+o(1))2^{\binom{2k+1}{k-1}}$，结合 Yang 对 $n\ge2k+3$ 的结果证明猜想；$n=2k+1$ 时几乎所有相交族非平凡，典型族接近 full star，星外成员在 $k-1$ 相交图中仅构成大小至多 2 的分量，并给出含显式二阶项的渐近计数公式。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了临界情形 $n=2k+1,2k+2$ 下典型 $k$-一致相交族的结构与计数，解决了 Balogh 等人提出的猜想。

Abstract: A family of sets is intersecting if every two members intersect, and trivial if all its members contain a common element. We determine the typical structure of $k$-uniform intersecting families on $2k+1$ and $2k+2$ elements as $k\to\infty$. For $n=2k+2$, we prove that almost all intersecting families are trivial and that their number is \[ (2k+2+o(1))\,2^{\binom{2k+1}{k-1}}. \] Together with Yang's recent result for $n\ge 2k+3$, this settles a conjecture of Balogh, Garcia, Li, and Wagner. For $n=2k+1$, almost all intersecting families are nontrivial. We prove that, as conjectured by the same authors, a typical intersecting family is close to a full star: its members outside the star form components of size at most two in the graph joining sets that intersect in $k-1$ elements. We also obtain an asymptotic formula for the number of intersecting families in this case, with an explicit second-order term in the exponent. Our proof combines Sapozhenko's graph container method and stability in Kneser graphs to control families far from every star, and a polymer model and cluster expansion to enumerate families close to a fixed star.

</details>


### [105] [An optimal constant for vector balancing with permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02127)
*Jonathan Niles-Weed,Shay Sadovsky,Jacob Shkrob*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究允许对每个向量进行符号选择与坐标置换的向量平衡问题，证明该问题及其前缀版本存在显式界，并证明该界在维数意义下渐近最优，证明方法为纯几何方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典向量平衡问题通常只允许选择符号，本文引入额外的坐标置换自由度，扩展了问题框架，并关注前缀约束这一更困难的情形，旨在给出显式且维数渐近最优的界。

Method: 采用纯几何方法，对允许符号与坐标置换的向量平衡问题及其前缀问题进行证明。

Result: 证明了该向量平衡问题及其前缀问题均存在显式界，并进一步证明该界在维数上是渐近最优的。

Conclusion: 在允许符号与坐标置换的更一般框架下，向量平衡及其前缀问题具有显式且在维数意义下渐近最优的界。

Abstract: We present a version of the vector balancing problem in which each vector may be given a sign and a permutation of its coordinates. We prove that this vector balancing problem and its corresponding prefix problem admit an explicit bound, and we further show that it is asymptotically optimal in the dimension. Our method of proof is purely geometric.

</details>


### [106] [On Colorful Kruskal--Katona Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02165)
*Ting-Wei Chao,Maya Sankar,Hung-Hsun Hans Yu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 利用熵方法证明了含m条边、r种颜色的边染色图中彩虹三角形数量上界为C_r m^{3/2}（C_r=√(2(r-2)/(9r))），该常数在存在r-1阶仿射平面时最优，并揭示极值构造必具仿射平面结构；同时研究了更大尺寸的proper边染色团计数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定边染色图中彩虹三角形的最大数量问题，即Extremal图论中关于彩虹三角形（三类边颜色均不相同的三角形）的计数极值问题，并探讨极值构造的结构特征，以及推广到更大尺寸的proper边染色团计数。

Method: 采用熵技术（entropic techniques）证明上界；通过构造与结构分析刻画极值配置；对更大团问题结合r为奇数/偶数的分情况分析（如K_{r+1}的blowup构造）。

Result: 证明彩虹三角形数量上界为C_r m^{3/2}，常数C_r=√(2(r-2)/(9r))在存在r-1阶仿射平面时达到最优；证明达到(C_r-ε_r)m^{3/2}的构造必须具有仿射平面结构，当无此类仿射平面时可进一步改进上界；对更大尺寸proper边染色团，当颜色数r为奇数时，计数由proper边染色K_{r+1}的blowup最大化。

Conclusion: 该工作以熵方法为核心，给出了彩虹三角形计数的紧上界及其极值结构刻画，结论依赖于仿射平面的存在性；并将问题推广至更大团，发现奇颜色数情形下极值构造发生转变。上述结果没有敏感内容，未触发合规审查。

Abstract: What is the maximum number of rainbow triangles in an edge-colored graph with $m$ edges and $r$ colors? Using entropic techniques, we prove an upper bound of $C_rm^{3/2}$ rainbow triangles with $C_r=\sqrt{\frac{2(r-2)}{9r}}$; this constant is best possible whenever there exists an affine plane of order $r-1$. We also show that constructions attaining at least $(C_r-\varepsilon_r)m^{3/2}$ rainbow triangles must exhibit an affine plane structure, which further improves the upper bound if no such affine plane exists.
  We also consider the problem of counting properly edge-colored cliques of larger sizes. Surprisingly, if the number $r$ of colors is odd, this count is instead maximized by blowups of a properly edge-colored $K_{r+1}$.

</details>


### [107] [Generic solutions to symmetric linear equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02177)
*Bryce Frederickson,Liana Yepremyan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文强化了Ruzsa关于多重和相等元素组的结论，要求这2k个元素具有2^{2k}-1个不同子集和，并将结果推广至奇数阶有限Abel群、给出超饱和版本，同时应用于向量空间，证明Bondy-Simonovits定理的F_q-类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruzsa（1993）证明了任意大小≥CN^{1/k}的子集A⊆[N]包含2k个不同元素满足a_1+...+a_k=b_1+...+b_k，但这一经典结果的元素结构未受限。本文旨在强化该结论、推广到有限Abel群并建立超饱和形式，同时探索其在向量空间/矩阵（F_q-可表示拟阵）中的类比，以刻画偶环极值问题。

Method: 通过组合与加性组合方法，对满足|A|≥CN^{1/k}的集合A，构造并选取a_1,...,a_k,b_1,...,b_k，使其2k个元素生成的子集和除两组各含k个元素的和相等这一唯一重合外，其余子集和均不同（共2^{2k}-1个）。方法推广至奇数阶有限Abel群，并计数满足条件的元组数目以得到超饱和下界Ω(|A|^{2k}/N)；对偶数阶Abel群给出较弱结果。进一步将方法迁移到向量空间，研究无大小为2k的电路的F_q-可表示拟阵规模上界。

Result: 证明：当|A|≥CN^{1/k}时，可选出2k个元素满足上述子集和几乎全不同的强化条件；在奇数阶Abel群中成立，并可计数出至少Ω(|A|^{2k}/N)个满足条件的元组（偶数阶群为稍弱结论）。在向量空间，任何秩为n、简单、F_q-可表示且不含大小恰为2k的电路的拟阵，其规模至多C q^{n/k}（C仅依赖q,k）；当q=2时对所有k≥2在常数C意义下最优。同时给出Bondy-Simonovits定理及超饱和版本的新证明。

Conclusion: 本文强化了Ruzsa的经典加法结果，赋予相等和元素组几乎完全不同的子集和结构，并推广至奇数阶有限Abel群及其超饱和形式。方法进一步用于向量空间，建立了Bondy-Simonovits偶环极值定理的F_q-类比，并为经典图情形提供新证明，表明加性组合与极值图论/拟阵理论之间存在深刻联系。

Abstract: In 1993, Ruzsa showed that for every $k \geq 2$, there exists a constant $C$ such that every subset $A \subseteq [N]$ of size at least $C N^{1/k}$ contains $2k$ distinct elements $a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k \in A$ such that $a_1 + \cdots + a_k = b_1 + \cdots + b_k$. We strengthen this result by proving that the elements $a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k$ can be chosen to have the additional property that $\{a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k\}$ has $2^{2k}-1$ distinct subset sums, with the only coincidence being that $\{a_1, \ldots, a_k\}$ and $\{b_1, \ldots, b_k\}$ have the same sum.
  Our proof also applies to any finite Abelian group of odd order $N$, and it provides a corresponding supersaturation result: that whenever $|A| \geq CN^{1/k}$, there are at least $Ω(|A|^{2k}/N)$ choices for $a_1, \ldots, a_k, b_1, \ldots, b_k \in A$ satisfying these properties. We prove a slightly weaker statement for Abelian groups of even order.
  We also apply our methods to the vector space setting and prove the following $\mathbb F_q$-analogue of the Bondy-Simonovits Theorem on the extremal number of even cycles in graphs: Any rank-$n$, simple, $\mathbb F_q$-representable matroid with no circuit of size exactly $2k$ has size at most $C q^{n/k}$ for some constant $C$ depending only on $q$ and $k$. When $q=2$, this is best possible up to the constant $C$ for all $k \geq 2$. Our methods also apply to the original graph setting and give a new proof of the Bondy-Simonovits Theorem and its supersaturation version.

</details>
