# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Spherical density for real semisimple groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00298)
*Christopher Lutsko*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 在半单实李群的余紧格塔上，弱单射半径性质等价于球密度假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨弱单射半径性质与球密度假设之间的关系，并在余紧阿基米德情形下建立二者的等价性。

Method: 利用谱壁附近的均匀球质量估计与正预迹核，不依赖 tempered 谱估计或先验谱间隙。

Result: 证明弱单射半径性质蕴含相同参数的球密度假设；结合 Golubev 与 Kamber 的逆定理，得到余紧阿基米德情形下的等价性。

Conclusion: 在余紧阿基米德设定下，弱单射半径性质与球密度假设等价。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected semisimple real Lie group with finite center and let $(Γ_N)$ be a tower of cocompact lattices. We prove that the weak injective radius property implies the spherical density hypothesis with the same parameter. Together with the converse theorem of Golubev and Kamber, this gives an equivalence in the cocompact Archimedean setting. The proof uses a uniform spherical-mass estimate near spectral walls and a positive pre-trace kernel, but no tempered-spectrum estimate or a priori spectral gap.

</details>


### [109] [Smooth perturbation theory and generic Cantor spectrum for multi-dimensional monotone quasiperiodic operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00733)
*Ilya Kachkovskiy,Leonid Parnovski,Roman Shterenberg*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究多维拟周期薛定谔算子（单调锯齿型势，扰动局域化区）。证明了给定谱隙闭合当且仅当对应本征函数在原点为零；由此得出对一般采样函数f谱为Cantor集且所有可能谱隙打开，并给出部分谱隙闭合的函数类，指出某些谱隙更难闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维拟周期薛定谔算子的谱结构（尤其是谱隙开闭与Cantor谱性质）在扰动局域化区尚缺乏刻画。作者希望建立谱隙闭合与本征函数在原点取零值之间的等价判据，从而判断谱隙的开闭及谱的Cantor性质。

Method: 考虑多维拟周期薛定谔算子，势为单调锯齿型，处于扰动局域化区。引入自然谱隙标号（gap labeling），将谱隙与相应本征函数在原点取值对应；通过分析该对应关系证明谱隙闭合的充要条件，并结合对采样函数f的一般性与特定构造进行论证。

Result: 证明了谱隙闭合当且仅当（经自然谱隙标号对应的）本征函数在原点为零。据此得出：对一般（generic）采样函数f，谱为Cantor集且所有可能谱隙均为开；同时构造出一类函数f使某些谱隙闭合；并观察到某些谱隙比其他谱隙更难闭合。

Conclusion: 谱隙闭合可由本征函数在原点是否为零完全刻画；一般情形下谱是Cantor集且全谱隙打开，但也存在使部分谱隙闭合的采样函数，且不同谱隙的闭合难度存在差异。

Abstract: We consider multi-dimensional quasiperiodic Schrödinger operators with monotone sawtooth-type potentials in the perturbative localization regime. We prove that a given gap is closed iff a corresponding (via a natural gap labeling) eigenfunction vanishes at the origin. Using this, we show that for a generic choice of the sampling function $f$, the spectrum of such an operator is a Cantor set, with all possible gaps being open. We also provide a class of functions $f$ for which some gaps are closed. Finally, we observe that certain gaps are more difficult to close than the others.

</details>


### [110] [Inverse spectral results for the magnetic Schrödinger operator in low dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01479)
*Devon Farrell,Anna Holloway,Nikhil Savale*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究一维与二维半经典磁Schrödinger算子的谱决定性问题：证明偶数一维半经典势可由Schrödinger算子谱决定，二维径向磁场可由纯磁Schrödinger算子谱决定，从而推广Guillemin-Wang的工作并肯定其猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱决定性问题（能否由算子谱唯一确定势/磁场）是逆谱理论的核心问题。Guillemin-Wang此前已开展相关研究并提出猜想，本文旨在解决该猜想并将结果推广到半经典磁Schrödinger算子情形。

Method: 在半经典框架下分别处理一维与二维情形：一维情形考察具有偶对称性的半经典势；二维情形考察径向磁场对应的纯磁Schrödinger算子。利用半经典谱分析技术建立谱与势/磁场参数之间的对应关系。

Result: 得到两个结论：（1）一维偶半经典势由该Schrödinger算子的谱唯一决定；（2）二维径向磁场由纯磁Schrödinger算子的谱唯一决定。

Conclusion: 本文的谱决定性结果推广了Guillemin-Wang的早期工作，并正面回答了其提出的猜想，表明在相应对称性条件下半经典磁Schrödinger算子的谱足以恢复势与磁场信息。

Abstract: We consider the semiclassical magnetic Schrödinger operator in dimensions one and two. The first result is that an even one dimensional semiclassical potential is spectrally determined by the Schrödinger operator. The second result states that a two dimensional radial magnetic field is spectrally determined by a purely magnetic Schrödinger operator. This extends the earlier work of Guillemin-Wang and answers a conjecture of theirs therein.

</details>


### [111] [Eigenvalues of Hermitian Toeplitz matrices with Fisher--Hartwig symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01498)
*Jacek Wszoła*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 研究由对称 Fisher-Hartwig 符号生成 Hermitian Toeplitz 矩阵的特征值，利用离散 Laplacian 的 Dirichlet-Neumann 分界法给出非渐近显式界，证明所有特征值单重，并对每个特征值给出带显式余项界的两项近似，将已有结果从 α>1 扩展到 α∈(0,2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于此类 Toeplitz 矩阵特征值的结果仅覆盖 α>1，对 α∈(0,1] 的区间存在空白；作者希望填补这一空白，覆盖完整的 α∈(0,2) 范围。

Method: 基于离散 Laplacian 的 Dirichlet-Neumann 分界（bracketing）技术，建立个体特征值的非渐近显式界；同时构造近似特征向量，用于推导每个特征值的两项近似及余项显式界。该近似特征向量构造此前未在此类问题中使用。

Result: 得到所有特征值的非渐近显式界；证明所有特征值均为单重（simple）；当矩阵规模趋于无穷时，给出每个特征值的两项近似并带显式余项界。

Conclusion: 将 Hermitian Toeplitz 矩阵特征值的已知结果从 α>1 推广到完整区间 α∈(0,2)，并引入新的近似特征向量构造方法。

Abstract: We investigate the eigenvalues of Hermitian Toeplitz matrices generated by the symmetric Fisher-Hartwig symbol $\hat a(z) = (1-z)^{α/2}(1-z^{-1})^{α/2}$ for $α\in (0,2)$. Using Dirichlet-Neumann bracketing of the discrete Laplacian, we establish explicit, non-asymptotic bounds for the individual eigenvalues. As a consequence, we prove that all eigenvalues are simple. We also obtain a two-term approximation for every eigenvalue, with explicit bounds on the remainder, as the size of the matrix tends to infinity. While known results are limited to $α> 1$, we bridge this gap by covering the full range of $α\in (0,2)$. Our approach uses a construction of approximate eigenvectors that has not previously been applied in this setting.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [Two-Line Elimination and the Weierstrass Group Law in Arbitrary Characteristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00028)
*Hongfeng Wu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 通过对多项式分解、形式微分与二次插值的组合，证明了广义Weierstrass形式椭圆曲线上弦-切线运算的结合律，且证明适用于任意特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦-切线运算（即椭圆曲线上的群运算）的结合律通常依赖几何论证或特定特征假设。本文旨在给出一个纯代数的、适用于任意特征的严格证明。

Method: 结合多项式分解、形式微分与二次插值。核心是消除相继加法 S=P⊕Q 与 T=S⊕R 中出现的两条非竖直线 Y=ℓ(X) 与 Y=m(X)，建立含参数 c 与首一二次多项式 h 的恒等式。切性通过Weierstrass方程的形式偏导数表达，从而不依赖特征。短Weierstrass形式另给简洁证明，将 q 与 Zwegers 构造中的插值抛物线等同。

Result: 得到恒等式 ℓ+m+Apol = c(X−x_S)，Gpol+ℓm = (X−x_S)h，以及 (Y−ℓ)(Y−m) = (X−x_S)(h−cY)+Y²+Apol(X)Y−Gpol(X)。当 c≠0 时，q=h/c 的图像经过 P、Q、R 与 −T。两种括号方式通过普通或 Hermite 插值确定同一二次多项式，故给出相同终点。

Conclusion: 弦-切线运算满足结合律，证明在任意特征下成立，并可纳入 Zwegers 插值抛物线框架。

Abstract: Let $K$ be a field and let $E/K$ be a nonsingular projective curve in general Weierstrass form. We prove associativity of its chord--tangent operation by combining polynomial factorization, formal differentiation, and quadratic interpolation. The main identity eliminates the two nonvertical lines $Y=\ell(X)$ and $Y=m(X)$ occurring in the successive additions $S=P\oplus Q$ and $T=S\oplus R$. There are $c\in K$ and a monic quadratic polynomial $h\in K[X]$ such that \[
  \ell+m+\Apol=c(X-x_S),
  \qquad
  \Gpol+\ell m=(X-x_S)h, \] and \[
  (Y-\ell)(Y-m)
  =(X-x_S)(h-cY)
  +Y^2+\Apol(X)Y-\Gpol(X). \] When $c\ne0$, the graph of $q=h/c$ contains $P,Q,R$, and $-T$. The two bracketings determine the same quadratic by ordinary or Hermite interpolation and therefore yield the same final point. The proof applies in every characteristic, since tangency is expressed through the formal partial derivatives of the Weierstrass equation. For the short Weierstrass form, a separate concise proof identifies $q$ with the interpolating parabola in Zwegers's construction.

</details>


### [113] [PageRank on Lubotzky--Phillips--Sarnak graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00109)
*Shenghao Hua*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 比较LPS图上独立几何时间停时的随机游走回归概率与无限正则树情形，用McKay测度给出树值、Ihara zeta函数刻画环导致的额外回归，并在特定尺度下估计该修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机游走在LPS图与正则树上的回归概率存在差异，其差由图中环（闭合路径）引起，需量化该修正，尤其在平均游走长度与最短环长相当时的临界尺度下。

Method: 以McKay测度为树的基准值，用Ihara zeta函数描述环带来的额外回归；对独立几何停时游走，在平均长度与最短环长可比的区间内估计修正项；在素数级上、乘以顶点数后，无条件证明含双对数因子的上界与有界固定矩；并在二次Dirichlet L函数的广义黎曼假设下分析其阶。

Result: 无条件情形下，乘以顶点数后的修正具有双对数因子上界及有界固定矩；在GRH（二次Dirichlet L函数）下，该修正与顶点数的倒数同阶。

Conclusion: LPS图上几何停时随机游走的回归概率修正由环结构主导，其尺度在GRH下精确至与顶点数倒数同阶，无条件结果给出较弱的双对数上界。

Abstract: We compare return probabilities for random walks stopped at an independent geometric time on Lubotzky--Phillips--Sarnak graphs and on the infinite regular tree, with the McKay measure giving the tree value and the Ihara zeta function describing the additional returns caused by cycles. We estimate this correction when the mean walk length is comparable to the length of the shortest cycle, a regime in which walks long enough to traverse a cycle receive non-negligible weight.
  Unconditionally, we prove an upper bound with a double logarithmic factor and bounded fixed moments over prime levels after multiplication by the number of vertices. Under the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis for quadratic Dirichlet $L$-functions, the correction is of the same order as the reciprocal of the number of vertices.

</details>


### [114] [Some Methods to Solve the Diophantine Equation ay^2=b(x^k-c^k)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00119)
*Xiaodong Zhuang,Nikos E. Mastorakis*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究丢番图方程 ay²=b(x^k−c^k)，针对不同 k 给出便于计算机实现的求解方法，将原方程降阶以提高整数解搜索效率，并给出 ay²=b(x^2n−c^2n) 不可解的一个充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 丢番图方程 ay²=b(x^k−c^k) 的整数解求解通常因次数高、搜索空间大而效率低下，缺乏针对不同 k 值、便于计算机算法实现的统一处理手段，因此需要降阶转化与不可解性判定工具。

Method: 按 k 的不同情形分类讨论，提出相应的求解方法；将原高次方程约化为低次方程，从而缩小整数解搜索范围、提升算法效率；并对 k=2n 的情形推导出方程不可解的充分条件。

Result: 获得针对不同 k 值的可算法化求解方案，实现原方程的降阶处理，并给出 ay²=b(x^2n−c^2n) 无整数解的一个充分条件。

Conclusion: 通过分类讨论与降阶转化，可有效提升此类丢番图方程整数解搜索的计算效率，所给不可解充分条件为算法中提前终止无解情形提供了判据。

Abstract: The solution of the Diophantine equation ay^2=b(x^k-c^k) is studied in different cases of k. Methods are proposed for different cases of k, which are convenient for computer algorithm implementation. The original equation is reduced to lower-degree equations, which can improve the efficiency in searching possible integer solutions. A sufficient condition of the unsolvability of ay^2=b(x^2n-c^2n) is presented.

</details>


### [115] [An explicit family of twists of the Carlitz module with unbounded analytic rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00379)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文否定了Grishkov-Logachev关于固定有限域上Carlitz模扭曲解析秩有界性的猜想，给出q≥3时解析秩无界的显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grishkov与Logachev在其综述（问题1.4）中提出：对固定有限域F_q，Carlitz模的各扭曲（twists）的解析秩是否有界。本文旨在回答该问题，并同时处理综述中问题3.6.1至3.6.4。

Method: 对q=p^s、n≥1定义P_{q,n}=(θ^{q^n}-θ)^{q-2}。研究Grishkov-Logachev矩阵M(P_{q,n},1,k)的有向图，显式刻画其回路（circuits）：回路两两不交、不涉及含t的项，并与集合E={l∈[0,q-2]: p∤(l+1)}中元素的n次幂E^n在循环旋转下的轨道一一对应。由此分析L(C_P,t,T)的因子分解与解析秩。

Result: L(C_P,t,T)不依赖于t，按每条轨道分解为单项1-wt(ν)T^{per(ν)}的乘积；解析秩等于权重为1的轨道上p^{v_p(per(ν))}之和。当n为p的幂时，该值等于(q/p)^n (p-1)^{n-1}，随n无界增长，从而对每个q≥3否定原猜想。

Conclusion: 对每个q≥3，Carlitz模扭曲的解析秩无界，否定了Grishkov-Logachev问题1.4的猜想；所用方法还回答了该综述问题3.6.1–3.6.4在此族情形下的结论。

Abstract: Grishkov and Logachev have asked (Problem 1.4 of their recent survey) whether, for a fixed finite field F_q, the analytic ranks of the twists of the Carlitz module are bounded. We answer this in the negative for every q >= 3. For q = p^s and n >= 1 put P_{q,n} = (theta^(q^n) - theta)^(q-2). We show that the directed graph of the matrix M(P_{q,n},1,k) of Grishkov-Logachev has a completely explicit set of circuits: they are pairwise disjoint, none of them meets an entry involving t, and they are in bijection with the orbits of E^n under cyclic rotation, where E is the set of l in [0,q-2] with p not dividing l+1. Consequently L(C_P, t, T) does not depend on t, it factors as a product of one term 1 - wt(nu) T^per(nu) per orbit, and the analytic rank equals the sum of p^(v_p(per(nu))) over the orbits of weight 1. When n is a power of p this equals (q/p)^n (p-1)^(n-1), which is unbounded. The argument also answers questions 3.6.1 to 3.6.4 of the survey for this family.

</details>


### [116] [New bounds for the integer Chebyshev constant of [0,1]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00386)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文将整数切比雪夫常数 t_Z([0,1]) 的区间从 [0.4213, 0.422685] 收紧至 [0.4222286000, 0.4226846975]，区间宽度由 1.39e-3 缩小到 4.56e-4，上下界均由显式证书给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自 Pritsker(2005) 与 Flammang(2014) 以来，[0,1] 上的整数切比雪夫常数长期被夹在 0.4213 与 0.422685 之间，区间较宽。作者希望借助可验证的显式证书进一步收紧该区间，提高数值估计的精度。

Method: 上下界均通过显式证书构造：上界由一个含 92 个因子及显式整数指数的整数多项式承载；下界由一个覆盖 947 个胞腔的离散测度与包含 206 个不可约多项式的库构成，并借助一个将整除性代价项显式化的不等式给出。作者另附两个自包含脚本，仅依赖这些证书即可重新推导出两界。

Result: 得到 0.4222286000 ≤ t_Z([0,1]) ≤ 0.4226846975，区间宽度由 1.39e-3 降至 4.56e-4，两个边界均由显式证书严格验证。

Conclusion: 该工作显著缩小了 [0,1] 上整数切比雪夫常数的已知区间，并通过证书加可复现脚本的形式提升了结果的可验证性与可信度，为后续进一步逼近该常数提供了新的基线。

Abstract: The integer Chebyshev constant of [0,1] has been bracketed by 0.4213 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.422685 since the work of Pritsker (2005) and of Flammang (2014). We prove
  0.4222286000 <= t_Z([0,1]) <= 0.4226846975,
  narrowing the interval from 1.39e-3 to 4.56e-4. Both bounds are established by explicit certificates. The upper bound is carried by an integer polynomial with 92 factors and explicit integer exponents; the lower bound by a discrete measure on 947 cells together with a library of 206 irreducible polynomials, through an inequality in which the terms paying for divisibility are made explicit. Two self-contained scripts, included as ancillary files, re-derive both bounds from the certificates alone.

</details>


### [117] [On a problem on Ramanujan-Sato series of level 11](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00551)
*John M. Campbell*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文解决了关于level 11 Ramanujan-Sato系数c_{11}(n)长期未解的显式表达问题，给出了双重二项式求和公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定level 11 Ramanujan-Sato级数系数c_{11}(n)的表达式是一个长期公开问题，Cooper等人在2015年论文、2017年教材及2025年综述中反复提出，期望得到与低层级Ramanujan-Sato级数系数公式直接类比的形式（固定、嵌套、有限的二项式求和），但此前一直未获解决。

Method: 作者引入并证明了一个针对c_{11}(n)的双重二项式求和公式（double binomial sum formula），该公式与低层级Ramanujan-Sato级数的系数公式形成直接类比。

Result: 成功给出了对所有非负整数n均成立的c_{11}(n)的双重二项式求和表达式，解决了该公开问题。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了level 11 Ramanujan-Sato系数显式公式的空白，实现了与低层级情形相一致的类比结构，从而回答了Cooper等人提出的长期悬而未决的问题。

Abstract: A longstanding open problem concerning Ramanujan-Sato series is given by determining an expression for the coefficients $c_{11}(n)$ associated with Ramanujan-Sato series of level 11 with a fixed, nested, finite binomial sum for all nonnegative integers $n$, by direct analogy with the known formulas for the coefficients for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series (and this is clarified and made explicit in our paper). This open problem was given by Cooper et al. in 2015 [J. Math. Anal. Appl.], in Cooper's 2017 textbook on Ramanujan's theta functions, and in Cooper's 2025 contribution to the Contemp. Math. series. This problem seems to have remained open. We succeed in solving this problem, by introducing and proving a double binomial sum formula for $c_{11}(n)$ that is directly analogous to the coefficient formulas for lower-level Ramanujan-Sato series.

</details>


### [118] [Perfect squares in reciprocal-sum sequences and primes that are inert in quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00765)
*Mateo Matijasevick,Santiago Rodríguez,Gregorio Salazar*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究正有理数递推序列 $x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+\cdots+x_n)$ 的化简分数 $x_n=a_n/b_n$，刻画何时存在常数 $c$ 使 $c a_n+b_n$ 恒为完美幂，并给出不变量与两个推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者关心递推序列化简分数的分子分母组合 $a_n,b_n$ 的算术性质，希望确定 $c a_n+b_n$ 恒为完美幂的充要条件，并揭示其背后的数论结构。

Method: 关键是把问题转化为 $t_n=(x_0+\cdots+x_n)/h$ 满足递推 $t_{n+1}=t_n+1/t_n$，利用该递推下约分不抵消的性质，导出 $c a_n+b_n$ 的精确公式；再借助二次域 $\Q(\sqrt{-k})$ 中的惯性条件分析不变量 $b_n^2-\frac4k a_n^2$，并将方法推广到含 $n\mu h$ 项的递推。

Result: 证明了：存在有理常数 $c>0$ 使对所有此类序列和所有 $n\ge2$ 都有 $c a_n+b_n$ 为完美幂，当且仅当 $k=h^2$ 且 $h$ 的每个素因子均 $\equiv3\pmod4$；此时 $c=2/h$ 且这些幂为平方。还给出 $c a_n+b_n$ 的精确公式，证明沿任一序列 $h$ 的每个素因子至多破坏一项。对任意 $k$，$b_n^2-\frac4k a_n^2$ 恒为有理平方，并恰在 $k$ 的平方部分的素因子于 $\Q(\sqrt{-k})$ 中满足惯性条件时为整数平方。推广情形涉及二次域 $\Q(\sqrt{\mu^2-4})$。

Conclusion: 该工作用二次域与递推不抵消现象完整刻画了 $c a_n+b_n$ 的完美幂性质，统一了实、虚二次域（包括 $\Q(\sqrt{-3})$）的情形，给出了两条自然推广。

Abstract: Let $k$ be a positive integer, and consider the sequences of positive rationals with $x_0\in\N$ and $x_{n+1}=k/(x_0+x_1+\dots+x_n)$. Write $x_n=a_n/b_n$ in lowest terms. We show that there is a rational constant $c>0$ such that $c\,a_n+b_n$ is a perfect power for every such sequence and every $n\ge2$ if and only if $k=h^2$ and every prime factor of $h$ is congruent to $3$ modulo $4$; in that case $c=2/h$ and the powers are squares. The proof rests on the observation that $t_n=(x_0+\dots+x_n)/h$ satisfies $t_{n+1}=t_n+1/t_n$, a recursion under which reduced fractions never cancel. This yields an exact formula for $c\,a_n+b_n$, shows that along any single sequence each prime factor of $h$ spoils at most one term, and leads to two generalizations. For arbitrary $k$ the invariant $b_n^2-\frac4k a_n^2$ is always a rational square, and it is always an integer square exactly when the primes dividing the square part of $k$ satisfy an inertness condition in $\Q(\sqrt{-k})$. For the recursions $x_{n+1}=h^2/(x_0+\dots+x_n+nμh)$ the role of the Gaussian integers is played by the quadratic fields $\Q(\sqrt{μ^2-4})$, which include $\Q(\sqrt{-3})$ and every real quadratic field.

</details>


### [119] [Congruence Jumping in Polynomial Divisibility Systems: Arithmetic Structure and Solution Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00808)
*Max A. Alekseyev,Dmitry I. Khomovsky*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究整数多项式系统 y|A(x)、x|B(y) 的非零整数解，提出"同余跳跃"方法及其对偶多项式规则，并给出二次情形与高阶例子的完整分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类双向整除系统在无互素、首一或单位假设下缺乏统一理论，尤其二次情形结构尚不清晰，需要新的变换工具和判据来刻画解集与无限链。

Method: 引入商变换（可改变多项式对）即"同余跳跃"；建立一般对偶多项式规则，无需互素/首一/单位假设，给出整数归一化的精确判据；用伴随曲面恒等式构造无限整数商链；二次情形用定量分母界、非整数圆锥参数的单侧无限链分类、Vieta 动力约化、非单位四循环与 Pell 型线性化；并用关系格判据将解集归约为有限因子搜索。

Result: 得到一般对偶多项式跳跃规则与可归一化判据；给出显式混合次数例子；二次有限链定量分母界；分类非整数圆锥参数的例外非恒定单侧无限链；证明二次状态变换约化为固定圆锥上的 Vieta 动力；构造无穷多正整数点的真实非单位四循环并给出一致 Pell 型线性化；分析一个非单位整除系统；关系格判据将某些解集化为有限因子搜索。

Conclusion: 同余跳跃为双向整除系统提供统一框架，二次情形的刚性通过 Vieta 动力、Pell 轨道与关系格完全刻画，并能构造无限链与周期结构。

Abstract: Let $A$ and $B$ be polynomials with integer coefficients. We study nonzero integer solutions of $$y\mid A(x),\qquad x\mid B(y),$$ using quotient transformations that may change the polynomial pair. We call this process \emph{congruence jumping}. We establish a general reciprocal-polynomial rule governing such jumps, without coprimality, monicity, or unit assumptions, and give exact criteria for when the resulting polynomial states can be normalized integrally. Companion-surface identities provide a mechanism for constructing infinite integral quotient chains, including an explicit mixed-degree example.
  The quadratic case is considerably more rigid. We obtain a quantitative denominator bound for finite chains, classify the exceptional nonconstant one-sided infinite chains with nonintegral conic parameter, and show that changing quadratic states reduce to ordinary Vieta dynamics on a fixed conic. We further construct a genuinely nonunit recurrent four-cycle with infinitely many positive integral points and show that its dynamics admits a uniform Pell-type linearization. A motivating nonunit divisibility system is analyzed through Pell orbits and changing-state ladders. Finally, an independent relation-lattice criterion reduces certain solution sets to a finite divisor search.

</details>


### [120] [Multiplicative recurrence of Piatetski--Shapiro and nil-Bohr sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00843)
*Sun-Kai Leung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文发展了Frantzikinakis--Klurman--Moreira的圆方法，给出了自然数集乘法回复性的充分判别准则，应用于小指数的Piatetski--Shapiro集与正则无理nil-Bohr集，并研究了幺模完全积性函数在其上的取值分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法回复性是乘性组合数论的核心概念之一，但一般自然数集的乘法回复性缺乏易用的判别工具；同时Piatetski--Shapiro集、nil-Bohr集等经典稠密集上的算术函数取值分布尚待刻画，需要统一的方法框架。

Method: 改造Frantzikinakis--Klurman--Moreira的圆方法，建立集合乘法回复性的充分准则；将该准则用于小指数的Piatetski--Shapiro集以及正则无理nil-Bohr集，并借助该框架分析幺模完全积性函数在这些集合上的取值分布。

Result: 得到了自然数集乘法回复性的充分准则；证明小指数Piatetski--Shapiro集与正则无理nil-Bohr集满足该准则，从而具有乘法回复性；并刻画了幺模完全积性函数沿这些集合的取值分布。

Conclusion: 扩展了圆方法在乘法回复性问题的适用范围，为Piatetski--Shapiro集与nil-Bohr集等集合的乘性结构及积性函数分布提供了统一处理途径。

Abstract: Adapting the circle method developed by Frantzikinakis--Klurman--Moreira, we establish a sufficient criterion for a set of natural numbers to be multiplicatively recurrent. We apply this criterion to Piatetski--Shapiro sets with small exponents and to regular irrational nil-Bohr sets. We also study the value distribution of unimodular completely multiplicative functions along these sets.

</details>


### [121] [Quadratic distances in even dimensions over prime fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01795)
*Thang Pham,Chun-Yen Shen,Dung The Tran,Boqing Xue*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究非退化二次型在有限域向量集上的距离集大小，给出下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上二次型诱导的距离集（差值的型值集合）的基数下界，推广经典加法组合学中的距离集问题到高维与一般二次型情形。

Method: 组合与加法组合学方法，考虑 Witt index 为 m-1 的非退化二次型 Q 在 F_p^{2m} 上，分析集合 E 的差集 Q(x-y) 的取值集合 Δ_Q(E)，给出基于 |E| 与 p^m 关系的下界估计，并单独处理平面情形 m=1。

Result: 当 |E|≥p^m 时，|Δ_Q(E)|≫ p/log(2+p^{m+1}/|E|)，隐含常数绝对且与 p,m,Q 无关；在 m=1 的平面情形，|Δ_Q(E)|≫ |E|/log(2|E|)，对 1≤|E|≤p 成立，且在对数因子意义下最优。

Conclusion: 证明了有限域上二次型距离集的一般下界，并在平面情形得到对数因子意义下最优的估计。

Abstract: Let $p$ be an odd prime, let $m\geq1$ be an integer, and let $Q$ be a nondegenerate quadratic form on $\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$ with Witt index $m-1$. For a nonempty set $E\subseteq\mathbb{F}_p^{2m}$, write $Δ_Q(E)=\{Q(x-y):x,y\in E\}$. We prove that, whenever $|E|\geq p^m$, $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg \frac{p}{\log\bigl(2+p^{m+1}/|E|\bigr)}, $$ with an absolute implied constant independent of $p$, $m$ and $Q$. In the planar case $m=1$, we also prove that $$ |Δ_Q(E)|\gg\frac{|E|}{\log(2|E|)}, \qquad (1\leq |E|\leq p), $$ which is optimal up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>


### [122] [Transcendence of dendric beta-expansions with pure discrete spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00856)
*Jihyuk Seo,Wolfgang Steiner*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明一类在代数基下具有β-展开的数在特定组合条件下是超越数，方法结合Rauzy分形与子空间定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究β-展开与超越数理论交叉问题，扩展已知超越性判别结果到更广的代换序列类。

Method: 利用Rauzy分形工具与子空间定理，在代数基β属于纯离散谱的dendric移位、且代换的指令序列满足组合条件时进行证明。

Result: 在所设组合条件下，此类数的β-展开对应的数是超越的；结果涵盖任意字母表上的Arnoux-Rauzy序列与Cassaigne-Selmer序列。

Conclusion: 将超越性结果推广至具有纯离散谱的dendric移位族，并表明Rauzy分形与子空间定理是处理该类问题的有效框架。

Abstract: We prove the transcendence of numbers that have $β$-expansions in algebraic bases $β$ belonging to dendric shifts with purely discrete spectrum, under certain combinatorial conditions on their directive sequences of substitutions. This includes Arnoux-Rauzy sequences on arbitrary alphabets and Cassaigne-Selmer sequences. The proof uses Rauzy fractals and the subspace theorem.

</details>


### [123] [Powers in orbits and semigroups generated by polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00893)
*Alina Ostafe,Dat T. Tran*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究数域上多项式（或由有限族多项式生成的半群）轨道中完全幂的存在性，修正并推广了Ahmadi等（2012）的结果，证明轨道中完全幂的乘性平移可通过有限算法检测，并给出了轨道中满足特定乘性关系的元素的有限性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ahmadi等（2012）此前给出了关于轨道中完全幂乘性平移的结果，但其证明存在错误且适用范围有限，作者希望修正该结果并将其推广到更一般的情形（多项式半群、任意数域），同时探究轨道中满足乘性关系的元素是否有限。

Method: 在数域上考虑由有限族多项式生成的半群所作用的轨道，采用代数数论与算术动力学方法，构造有限算法来检测轨道中完全幂的乘性平移，并对满足特定乘性关系的轨道元素进行分析以证明其有限性。

Result: 证明了轨道中完全幂的乘性平移可被有限算法检测（修正并推广了Ahmadi等人的结论），并给出了轨道中满足某些乘性关系的元素的有限性结果。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了算术动力学中关于完全幂与乘性平移的可判定性理论，将单多项式轨道的结果推广至多项式半群，并建立了轨道元素乘性关系的有限性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the presence of perfect powers in orbits of polynomials, or of semigroups generated by a finite family of polynomials, defined over a number field. In particular, we correct and generalize a result of Ahmadi et al. (2012), showing that multiplicative shifts of perfect powers in orbits can be detected by a finite algorithm. We also give a finiteness result on elements in orbits satisfying certain multiplicative relations.

</details>


### [124] [Construction of parametrized families of general pseudo-bimagic and bimagic squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00915)
*Ajai Choudhry*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了6×6伪双魔方与8×8双魔方的参数化族，并进一步构造了6m阶伪双魔方与8m阶双魔方的多参数族，同时给出具体数值实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 除了一族6×6伪双魔方外，此前尚无已发表的参数化双魔方族；作者希望填补这一空白，系统给出伪双魔方与双魔方的参数化构造。

Method: 通过构造6×6伪双魔方和8×8双魔方的参数化族，并利用这些族对任意正整数m进行扩展，构造出6m阶伪双魔方和8m阶双魔方的多参数族。

Result: 得到了6×6伪双魔方与8×8双魔方的参数化族，以及6m阶伪双魔方和8m阶双魔方的多参数族；并给出了6×6、12×12伪双魔方和8×8双魔方的数值例子。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了双魔方与伪双魔方的参数化构造，为更高阶此类魔方的构造提供了通用框架和具体实例。

Abstract: A magic square of order $n$ is a square array of $n^2$ distinct integers such that the integers of every row, every column and the two diagonals have the same common sum. If a magic square remains a magic square even when each integer is replaced by its square, it is called bimagic. If, however, all the rows and all the columns of the aforementioned square of squares add up to a common sum, but the two diagonals do not, the original magic square is called pseudo-bimagic. Except for one family of $6 \times 6$ pseudo-bimagic squares, parametrized families of bimagic squares have not been published. In this paper we construct parametrized families of $6 \times 6$ pseudo-bimagic squares and $8 \times 8$ bimagic squares. We use these families to construct, for any positive integer $m$, multi-parameter families of pseudo-bimagic squares of order $6m$ and bimagic squares of order $8m$. We also give numerical examples of $6 \times 6$ and $12 \times 12$ pseudo-bimagic squares and an $8 \times 8$ bimagic square.

</details>


### [125] [Baire-category transfer between Engel and Pierce expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00946)
*Min Woong Ahn*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了Engel展开与Pierce展开数字序列之间的显式对应（将第n位Engel展开数字加n-2），证明其诱导从单位区间无理数集到该集某可数稠密子集补集上的同胚，并给出Baire纲的转移原理、Borel类的精确保持情况；将该同胚识别为Moroz(2027)两个映射的复合，研究第二映射的连续性，确定间断点集、证明每个间断为跳跃且映射处处非单调，从而验证Moroz猜想；应用于刻画使Pierce展开数字级数在剩余集上发散的非增位置依赖权重，并关联两种展开数字序列的收敛指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Engel展开与Pierce展开是两类经典的数字展开系统，二者数字序列之间是否存在自然的显式对应，以及这种对应能否传递拓扑与测度性质（如Baire纲、Borel类），此前缺乏系统研究。Moroz(2027)提出了一个将修改Engel展开映到Pierce展开的映射并对其连续性、非单调性作出猜想，需要予以证明。文章旨在建立两种展开间的转移原理并解决这些公开问题。

Method: 构造显式数字对应：对第n位Engel展开数字加n-2得到对应的Pierce展开数字；证明该对应诱导从单位区间无理数集到某可数稠密子集补集的同胚；借助同胚分析Baire纲转移与Borel类的保持；将该同胚分解为Moroz(2027)所考虑的两个映射的复合，进而分析第二个映射的连续性，确定其间断点集并证明每个间断为跳跃、映射处处非单调；最后将结论应用于位置依赖权重级数发散性与收敛指数。

Result: 得到Engel与Pierce展开之间的显式同胚对应，并给出二者间的Baire纲转移原理及精确保持的Borel类；识别该同胚为Moroz(2027)两映射的复合；确定第二映射的间断点集，证明所有间断均为跳跃且该映射处处非单调，从而验证了Moroz关于非单调性及在例外集外连续的猜想；刻画了使Pierce展开数字级数在剩余集上发散的非增位置依赖权重，并建立了Engel与Pierce展开数字序列收敛指数之间的关系。

Conclusion: 文章在Engel与Pierce展开之间建立了显式同胚对应与Baire纲转移原理，完整解决了Moroz(2027)关于相关映射连续性与非单调性的猜想，并给出权重级数发散与收敛指数方面的应用，为两类数字展开的拓扑与度量性质比较提供了统一框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct an explicit correspondence between Engel and Pierce expansion digit sequences, obtained by adding $n-2$ to the $n$th Engel expansion digit, and show that it induces a homeomorphism from the set of irrationals in the unit interval onto the complement of a countable dense subset of this set. This yields a transfer principle for Baire category between the two expansions, and we determine exactly which Borel classes are preserved. We then identify this homeomorphism with the composition of two maps considered by Moroz (2027), and study the continuity of the second map, which sends modified Engel expansions to Pierce expansions. We determine its set of discontinuities, show that every discontinuity is a jump, and prove that the map is nowhere monotone. In particular, this confirms, for this map, the nowhere monotonicity and the continuity outside the exceptional set conjectured by Moroz (2027). As applications, we characterize the non-increasing position-dependent weights for which the associated series of Pierce expansion digits diverges on a comeager set, and we relate the convergence exponents of the Engel and Pierce expansion digit sequences.

</details>


### [126] [Indefinite theta vectors and generalized Nahm sums of arbitrary rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00967)
*Dandan Chen,Siyu Yin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了任意秩 r≥2 的一族带符号广义 Nahm 和的不定 theta 表示，并系统给出了其模变换、非全纯修正与乘积恒等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带符号广义 Nahm 和（尤其是任意秩情形）的 theta 表示与模性质，是连接 Nahm 和恒等式、theta 向量与模形式理论的核心问题。本文旨在为这类和式建立统一的高秩 theta 向量框架，并给出其解析补全与模变换规律。

Method: 对带符号广义 Nahm 和进行显式归一化与边界修正，使一支带符号分支给出与签名 (1,1) 格关联的 (2r-1) 分量全纯 theta 向量的偶数分量，欧拉型多重和给出奇数分量；利用 Zwegers 理论研究实解析补全的权一模变换，给出 S 变换的显式有限正弦矩阵；将非全纯修正分解为一元 theta 函数与一维误差函数级数的有限乘积和，并计算补全的反全纯导数；进一步对两支带符号分支的线性组合求值为 Jacobi 乘积商之差，并结合可对称化矩阵两族及其对偶的求值给出乘积向量各分量的多重和表示。

Result: 得到在秩与分量指标上统一的分量公式；确定实解析补全的权一模变换律及显式 S 变换正弦矩阵；给出非全纯修正的有限分解与反全纯导数；由完成变换律导出全纯向量的非齐次变换律；将两支带符号分支的线性组合求值为 Jacobi 乘积商之差，并对两族可对称化矩阵及其对偶给出四个相关乘积向量每个分量的多重和表示，这些表示为规定矩阵与对称化子的广义 Nahm 和的有限组合，在对偶族中允许带符号和与半周期平移。

Conclusion: 本文建立了任意秩带符号广义 Nahm 和的统一 theta 向量理论，涵盖显式分量公式、权一模变换、非全纯修正分解及乘积恒等式，并推广到可对称化矩阵家族及其对偶，为 Nahm 和恒等式与模形式联系提供了系统性框架。

Abstract: We construct indefinite theta representations for a family of signed generalized Nahm sums of arbitrary rank \(r\geq2\). After explicit normalization and boundary correction, one signed branch gives the even components of a \((2r-1)\)-component holomorphic theta vector associated with a lattice of signature \((1,1)\). Euler-type multiple sums provide the odd components, yielding formulas uniform in both the rank and the component index. Using Zwegers' theory, we determine the weight-one modular transformation laws of the real-analytic completion, with an explicit finite sine matrix for the \(S\)-transformation. We decompose the nonholomorphic correction into a finite sum of products of unary theta functions and one-dimensional error-function series, and compute the antiholomorphic derivative of the completion. Together with the completed transformation laws, these formulas give the inhomogeneous transformation law of the holomorphic vector. We also evaluate a linear combination of two signed branches as a difference of Jacobi product quotients. This identity and further evaluations for two families of symmetrizable matrices and their duals, combined with known results, give multiple-sum representations for every component of four associated product vectors. These representations are finite combinations of generalized Nahm sums with the prescribed matrices and symmetrizers; in the dual families, signed sums and half-period translates are allowed.

</details>


### [127] [The sixth moment of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01035)
*Alexandre de Faveri,Mayank Pandey*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了黎曼zeta函数在临界线上的新的大值估计，首次改进了自Hardy-Littlewood(1923)以来关于$|\zeta(1/2+it)|\geq T^{1/8}$的测度上界，并由此改进了$4<k\leq 12$时zeta的$k$阶矩上界，特别给出了六阶矩估计$\int_0^T|\zeta(1/2+it)|^6dt\ll_\varepsilon T^{5/4-1/60+\varepsilon}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界线上zeta函数的大值估计及矩估计是解析数论的核心问题。自Hardy-Littlewood(1923)以来，$|\zeta(1/2+it)|\geq T^{1/8}$的测度上界长期未获改进，且$k$阶矩（尤其$4<k\leq 12$区间）的上界也有改进空间。作者旨在突破这一长期停滞，获得更优的大值测度界与矩界。

Method: 核心工具是作者新建立的、针对具有平方根相位的指数和及其扰动的的大值估计。这类指数和出现在zeta短二阶矩的展开过程中，作者利用该新估计来推出临界线上zeta的大值估计，并进而导出各阶矩的上界。

Result: 证明了临界线上zeta函数的新大值估计，首次改进Hardy-Littlewood(1923)关于$t\in[T,2T]$中满足$|\zeta(1/2+it)|\geq T^{1/8}$的测度上界；由此得到对每个$4<k\leq 12$的$k$阶矩的改进上界，特别地$\int_0^T|\zeta(1/2+it)|^6dt\ll_\varepsilon T^{5/4-1/60+\varepsilon}$。

Conclusion: 通过引入平方根相位指数和及其扰动的新大值估计，作者在临界线上zeta的大值测度与$k$阶矩（$4<k\leq 12$）方面取得了突破性改进，打破了自1923年以来$T^{1/8}$大值测度界的停滞局面。

Abstract: We prove new large value estimates for the Riemann zeta function on the critical line. For instance, we improve the upper bound on the measure of $t\in [T, 2T]$ with $|ζ(1/2 + it)| \geq T^{1/8}$ for the first time since Hardy-Littlewood (1923). Our results imply improved upper bounds on the $k$-th moment of zeta for every $4 < k \leq 12$. We show in particular that
  \begin{equation*}
  \int_{0}^{T} |ζ(\tfrac{1}{2} + it)|^6 \,d t \ll_\varepsilon T^{\frac{5}{4} - \frac{1}{60} + \varepsilon}.
  \end{equation*}
  The main ingredient is a new large value estimate for exponential sums with square-root phases, and certain perturbations. Those arise in the expansion of the short second moment of zeta.

</details>


### [128] [Translation invariant quadratic forms in $8$ variables and dense sets of primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01091)
*Zhaochen Pei,Maoru Tan,Weiwen Zhang,Lilu Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本论文研究翻译不变的不定二次型Q在8个素数上的零点问题：只要素数子集A在[1,X]内足够稠密（密度≫(log log X)^{-c}），就存在A中两两不同的素数p₁,…,p₈使得Q(p₁,…,p₈)=0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究属于素数与二次型的交叠问题（如Vinogradov型定理、Green-Tao-Ziegler的线性型素数定理的自然推广），旨在将Green近期的相关方法推广到无正定性假设的二次型情形，从而在更广泛的素量子集上获得变元二次方程的非平凡素数解。

Method: 推广和发展Green的近作，结合Hasse原理/局部-整体原则处理不定二次型的代数几何条件，并利用素数子集的密度条件≫(log log X)^{-c}通过解析数论（可能涉及圆法、筛法及W-trick）构造素变量解。

Result: 在对二次型Q施加温和条件（mild condition，可能涉及非退化/不定性等局部可解性假设）下，证明了：只要|A|/(X/log X)≫_Q (log log X)^{-c}（c>0为绝对常数），就存在两两不同的素数p₁,…,p₈∈A使得Q(p₁,…,p₈)=0。

Conclusion: 该结果将Green的相关工作推广至8变量不定二次型，得到了在相对稀疏（对数对数幂次稀疏）素数子集中依然存在二次型素数零点解的条件，扩展了素变量二次型方程可解性的适用范围。

Abstract: Let $Q$ be a translation invariant indefinite quadratic form in $8$ variables. Let $\mathcal{P}$ be the set of all primes. Let $\mathcal{A}\subset\mathcal{P}\cap\{1,\dots,X\}$. By developing the recent work of Green, we prove that subject to a mild condition on the form $Q$ there exist pairwise distinct primes $p_{1},\dots,p_{8}\in \mathcal{A}$ satisfying $Q(p_{1},\dots,p_{8})=0$ provided that \[\frac{|\mathcal{A}|}{X/\log X}\gg_{Q}(\log\log X)^{-c}\] for some absolute constant $c>0$.

</details>


### [129] [Sharp $p$-adic Extrema and Least Extremal Rows for Restricted Binomial GCDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01328)
*John Fairfax-Ball*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究受限二项式最大公约数 G(N;m)=gcd{C(N,k):0<k<N, m|k} 的 p-adic 赋值上确界，证明其为 r_p(m)（满足 m<p^r 的最小 r），给出构造性取等行；并对 m=p^a+1 (a≥2) 精确确定最小极值行 T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限二项式最大公约数族在文献中已知，但其 p-adic 赋值的确切上确界与最小取等行尚未被精确刻画；本文旨在给出 v_p(G(N;m)) 在可行行 N 上的最大值及取等结构，并厘清与已有结果的关系。

Method: 结合 Kummer 进位定理与显式的首位数字见证给出普适上界；用乘法阶构造实现取等；对 m=p^a+1 通过低于 p^{3a}+1 的严格极小性论证，并针对唯一最差首位数字模式给出备用见证。

Result: 对 p∤m 的素数 p，v_p(G(N;m)) 在一切可行行 N 上的最大可能值恰为 r_p(m)（m<p^r 的最小正整数 r），且存在构造性取等行；对 m=p^a+1（a≥2），最小极值行为 T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1。

Conclusion: 受限二项式最大公约数的 p-adic 赋值上确界由 r_p(m) 精确刻画，取等可由构造实现；特殊族 m=p^a+1 的最小极值行被完全确定。文中明确说明了与早期结果的关系及文献检索的局限。

Abstract: For integers $m\ge 2$ and rows $N>m$ divisible by $m$, consider the restricted binomial greatest common divisor $G(N;m)=\gcd\{\binom Nk:0<k<N,\ m\mid k\}$. Fix a prime $p$ with $p\nmid m$, and let $r_p(m)$ be the least positive integer $r$ such that $m<p^r$. We prove that the largest possible value of $v_p(G(N;m))$, as $N$ ranges over all admissible rows, is exactly $r_p(m)$, and we give a constructive equality row. Attainment is established before the least extremal row $T_p(m)$ is defined. For the special family $m=p^a+1$ with $a\ge2$, we determine that least row exactly: $T_p(p^a+1)=p^{3a}+1$. The proof combines Kummer's carry theorem with an explicit leading-digit witness for the universal upper bound, a multiplicative-order construction for equality, and a separate strict-minimality argument below $p^{3a}+1$ with a fallback witness for the unique worst leading-digit pattern. The selected-GCD family itself is known in the literature; the relation to earlier results and the limits of the documented literature search are stated explicitly.

</details>


### [130] [A Prime-Power Dichotomy for Nekrasov--Okounkov Hook Lengths and $t$-Core Partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01422)
*Meenu Sharma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文证明了Nekrasov–Okounkov钩长生成函数系数 $a_t(n)$ 与 $t$-core 分拆生成函数系数 $b_t(n)$ 之间的同余等价性当且仅当 $t$ 为素数幂，从而精确刻画了这两个系数序列模 $t$ 算术等价的模数类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nekrasov–Okounkov 钩长生成函数与 $t$-core 分拆生成函数均源自分拆理论与表示论，二者系数之间存在潜在同余关系。论文旨在精确判定使 $a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t$ 对所有 $n$ 成立的模数 $t$ 的完整刻画，从而揭示两序列算术等价的确切范围。

Method: 以 $a_t(n)$ 记 $F_{t^2}(x)$ 的系数、$b_t(n)$ 记 $C_t(x)$ 的系数，通过研究两生成函数系数在模 $t$（以及素数 $t=p$ 时模 $p^2$）下的同余行为，结合素数幂与非素数幂情形的区分进行证明，并以 $t=6$ 等非素数幂例子说明同余失效。

Result: 证明了尖锐的素数幂二分性：对任意整数 $t\ge 2$，同余 $a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t$ 对所有 $n\ge 0$ 成立当且仅当 $t$ 为素数幂；当 $t=p$ 为素数时同余可加强为模 $p^2$；对 $t=6$ 及所有非素数幂的 $t$，该同余不成立。

Conclusion: 素数幂恰好是使 Nekrasov–Okounkov 钩长系数序列与 $t$-core 分拆系数序列模 $t$ 算术等价的精确模数；该结果给出了两序列同余关系的完整分类，并强化了素数情形的同余精度至模 $p^2$。

Abstract: Let $a_t(n)$ denote the coefficients of the Nekrasov--Okounkov hook length generating function $F_{t^2}(x)$, and let $b_t(n)$ denote the coefficients of the $t$-core partition generating function $C_t(x)$. We prove a sharp prime-power dichotomy: for every integer $t\ge 2$, the congruence $$ a_t(n)\equiv b_t(n)\pmod t $$ holds for all $n\ge 0$ if and only if $t$ is a prime power. When $t=p$ is prime, the congruence strengthens to modulo $p^2$. For $t=6$, and more generally for every integer that is not a prime power, the congruence fails. This identifies prime powers as the exact moduli for which these two partition-theoretic coefficient sequences are arithmetically equivalent.

</details>


### [131] [Tridimensional character sums with polynomial arguments and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01524)
*Étienne Fouvry,Igor E. Shparlinski,Ping Xi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究带Dirichlet特征的加权三重特征和,在两类情形下给出非平凡上界,并应用于短区间内2×2不可约特征多项式矩阵的计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源自Ganguly与Rajan(2023)关于短区间内2×2矩阵(特征多项式不可约)计数的中间结果,需要更短区间上的估计。

Method: 对形如∑_{a~A}∑_{b~B}∑_{c~C} α(a,b)β(c)χ(f(a)+bc)的特征和,在β≡1、k=2,3,4,5且A,B,C>p^{1/8+ε},或β一般、k=2,3且A,B,C>p^{1/6+ε}时,建立非平凡上界。

Result: 在上述两类情形下均得到非平凡上界,从而可将相应矩阵计数推广到更短区间。

Conclusion: 改进的界扩展了原始应用范围,给出更短区间内此类矩阵的计数结果。

Abstract: Let $p$ be a large prime and $χ$ a non-trivial Dirichlet character modulo $p$. We study the character sum \[ \sum_{a \sim A} \sum_{b \sim B} \sum_{c \sim C} α(a,b) β(c)χ(f(a) + bc), \] where $z \sim Z$ means $Z\le z < 2Z$, $f \in \mathbb{Z}[X]$ is of small degree $k$, and $\boldsymbolα=(α(a,b))_{a\sim A,b\sim B}$ and $\boldsymbolβ=(β(c))_{c\sim C}$ are two complex coefficients. We prove non-trivial upper bounds for this sum in either of the two cases: (1) $\boldsymbolβ\equiv1$, $k=2,3,4,5$ and $A,B,C>p^{\frac{1}{8}+\varepsilon}$, (2) $\boldsymbolβ$ general, $k=2,3$ and $A,B,C>p^{\frac{1}{6}+\varepsilon}$, where $\varepsilon>0$ is fixed.
  This work was originally motivated by an intermediate result of Ganguly and Rajan (2023) on counting $2\times2$ matrices over $\mathbb{F}_p$ with irreducible characteristic polynomials, where the entries are in short segments. The new bounds here allow us to count such matrices in much shorter segments.

</details>


### [132] [Uniform estimate of Lang-Weil type for counting points of schemes over finite rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01748)
*Kien Huu Nguyen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限环 $\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z}$ 上有限型 $\mathbb{Z}$-概型的点数，建立了 $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z})$ 关于 $m$、$X(\mathbb{Z}/p^{m-1}\mathbb{Z})$ 及 p-adic Igusa 局部 zeta 函数极点的估计，可视为 Lang-Weil 估计的归纳版本，并与 Igusa 指数和猜想及 Cluckers-Mustaţǎ-作者 2019 年结果相呼应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Igusa 关于指数和的猜想以及 Lang-Weil 估计的启发，本文旨在对有限环 $\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z}$ 上概型的点数给出一个在 $m\geq 2$ 与 $p$ 上一致的估计，并以此逼近 Igusa 指数和猜想。

Method: 除使用奇点消解与 Denef 关于 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数的公式等常规工具外，作者还利用 Hermite 插值公式研究 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数的分子；并通过大量 p-adic 分析，用适当的广义对数典范阈值给出 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数非平凡极点实部的上界。

Result: 建立了 $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z})$ 在 $m\geq 2$ 与 $p$ 上一致、以 $m$、$X(\mathbb{Z}/p^{m-1}\mathbb{Z})$ 和 p-adic Igusa 局部 zeta 函数极点为表达的估计。当 $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ 不是仅有有理奇点的局部完全交时，该估计与 Igusa 指数和猜想的预期界一致；当 $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ 为仅有有理奇点的局部完全交时，该估计非常接近预期界。

Conclusion: 本文给出了有限环上概型点数计数的归纳式 Lang-Weil 型估计，推广并类比了 Cluckers-Mustaţǎ-作者 2019 年关于非有理奇点多项式指数和的结果；同时借助同一方法在有理奇点情形下逼近 Igusa 指数和猜想，并给出了 Igusa 局部 zeta 函数非平凡极点实部的上界。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the number of points of $\mathbb{Z}$-schemes $X$ of finite type over the finite rings $\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z}$ for positive integers $m$ and prime numbers $p$. Motivated by Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums and the Lang-Weil estimate, we establish an estimate of $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^m\mathbb{Z})$ in terms of $m$, $X(\mathbb{Z}/p^{m-1}\mathbb{Z})$ and poles of the $p$-adic Igusa local zeta function of $X$ uniformly in $m\geq 2$ and $p$. Our estimate might be viewed as an inductive version for counting points of schemes over finite rings of the Lang-Weil estimate. Our estimate agrees with the expected bound of Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums if $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ is not a locally complete intersection having only rational singularities. This can be regarded as an analogous version of the estimate of exponential sums modulo powers of primes of polynomials with non-rational singularities given by Cluckers, Mustaţǎ and the author in 2019. In the case that $X_{\mathbb{Q}}$ is a locally complete intersection having only rational singularities, our estimate is very close to the expected bound of Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums. To prove our estimate, beside using the usual tools such as resolutions of singularities and Denef's formula for Igusa local zeta functions, we also use the Hermit interpolation formula to study the numerators of Igusa local zeta functions. By the same method, we are able to approach Igusa's conjecture for exponential sums in the case of rational singularities. Lastly, to have further applications of our result in the future, we study heavily on $p$-adic analysis to give an upper bound of the real parts of non-trivial poles of Igusa local zeta functions in terms of suitable generalized log canonical thresholds.

</details>


### [133] [Infinite free pro-p products as absolute Galois groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01966)
*Tamar Bar-On*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Mel'nikov判别法的一个推广形式（Haran亦曾证明），并利用该判别法将自由射有限群与pro-p群层自由pro-p积的实现联系起来，研究其作为绝对Galois群的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Mel'nikov判别法是判定绝对Galois群的重要工具，但其适用范围有限。作者希望推广该判别法，以便处理更一般的群构造（如自由射有限积、自由pro-p积以及群层结构），从而扩大可用于构造绝对Galois群的群类。

Method: 证明推广版本的Mel'nikov判别法（该判别法Haran亦独立证明），然后以该判别法为工具，研究自由射有限群和pro-p群层（sheaf）的自由pro-p积作为绝对Galois群的实现问题。

Result: 得到了推广的Mel'nikov判别法，并据此建立了自由射有限群与pro-p群层的自由pro-p积在绝对Galois群可实现性方面的联系。

Conclusion: 推广的Mel'nikov判别法有效扩展了原有判别工具的适用性，为研究更复杂的pro-p群结构（含群层积）作为绝对Galois群的实现提供了统一途径。

Abstract: In this paper we prove a generalized version of Mel'nikov's Criterion, which has also been proved by Haran, and use it in order to connect the realization as absolute Galois groups of free pronite and free pro-p products of a sheaf of pro-p groups.

</details>


### [134] [On the prime geodesic theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01998)
*Alberto Acosta Reche*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 改进了模群以及更一般地任意不定四元数代数上同余子群的素数测地线定理误差项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 素数测地线定理的误差项估计是数论与双曲几何交叉领域的经典问题，模群情形下已有结果仍有改进空间，且可推广至四元数代数情形。

Method: 摘要未给出具体技术细节，仅说明构造了适用于模群及任意不定四元数代数同余子群的一般性方法。

Result: 得到模群素数测地线定理误差项的改进估计，并将其推广到任意不定四元数代数上的任意同余子群。

Conclusion: 结果提升了模群情形的误差项精度，并在四元数代数同余子群这一更广框架下成立，具有一般性。

Abstract: We improve the error term in the prime geodesic theorem for the modular group. More generally, our result applies to any congruence subgroup of any indefinite quaternion algebra over $\mathbb{Q}$.

</details>


### [135] [On The Index of Polynomial Compositions over Valued Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02111)
*Anuj Jakhar,Ravi Kalwaniya,Shanta Laishram,Prabhakar Yadav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究复合多项式 $f(h(x))$ 的单演性，其中 $h(x)$ 为三项式、系数取自任意 Krull 赋值环，给出了复合多项式判别式的显式公式，并据此刻画其生成单演域的条件，进而推广到多项式迭代与二项式迭代的单演性判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数数域是否具有幂整基（即是否单演）是经典问题，但对由多项式复合与动力迭代定义的域，已有方法难以处理。三项式与迭代结构在数域判别式和单演性研究中具有特殊重要性，但缺乏系统刻画，因此本文试图通过复合与迭代结构给出显式判别式和单演性判别准则。

Method: 在任意 Krull 赋值环上，对 $f(h(x))$（$f$ 首一、$h$ 为首一三项式）推导复合多项式判别式的显式公式；利用该公式将复合的单演性与 $f(x)$ 的单演性以及 $h(x)$ 临界值的显式无平方条件联系起来；进一步推广到多项式迭代情形，建立二项式迭代的单演性判别准则。

Result: 得到复合多项式判别式的显式公式；给出 $f(h(x))$ 生成单演域的充要条件，将复合单演性归结为 $f$ 的单演性与 $h$ 临界值的无平方条件；推广至多项式迭代并给出二项式迭代的单演性判别，由此构造出新的无限族单演域；此外给出定量结果并通过若干例子说明判别准则。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式判别式公式系统刻画了首一三项式复合与迭代的单演性，建立了复合单演性与因子多项式单演性、临界值无平方条件之间的联系，并得到二项式迭代的新无限族单演域，为研究多项式复合与动力迭代定义的数域单演性提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Determining whether an algebraic number field admits a power integral basis is a classical problem, but it can be difficult for fields defined by polynomial compositions and dynamical iterates. In this paper, we study the monogeneity of compositions $f(h(x))$, where $f(x)$ and $h(x)$ are monic polynomials over an arbitrary Krull valuation ring and $h(x)$ is a trinomial. We derive explicit formulas for the discriminant of the composition and use them to characterize when $f(h(x))$ generates a monogenic field. In particular, we relate the monogeneity of the composition to that of $f(x)$ and to explicit square-free conditions on the critical values of $h(x)$. We further extend the results to polynomial iteration and obtain a criterion for the monogeneity of binomial iterates, yielding new infinite families of monogenic fields. Finally, we give quantitative results and illustrate our criteria with several examples.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Minimal Wittstock Envelopes of Hermitian Completely Bounded Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00114)
*Mohsen Kian*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究Hermitian完全有界映射的完全正对称优控元（Wittstock包络）结构，证明每个Wittstock包络确定其下方唯一的最小包络，但最小包络未必唯一；给出最小优控元存在的中心支撑判据及有限维Choi表示刻画，并定义包络相对模与透视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermitian完全有界映射一般不存在绝对值的规范完全正类比，需要寻找合适的替代结构（完全正对称优控元/包络）来刻画其"正性"与模。

Method: 通过研究完全正对称优控元的序集E(Φ)（即Wittstock包络）；证明每个包络下方存在最小包络；利用谱投影的中心支撑给出最小优控元存在性判据；在有限维情形使用Choi表示进行刻画；用包络相对模定义Hermitian完全有界映射的包络相对透视。

Result: 每个Wittstock包络规范地确定其下方的最小Wittstock包络，但最小包络不一定唯一；给出在固定包络下方最小优控元存在的判据（基于谱投影的中心支撑）；有限维中，最小完全正优控元存在当且仅当Φ或-Φ完全正；定义了包络相对透视，推广Okayasu的完全正映射透视，在完全正情形退化为通常透视，且一般依赖所选Wittstock包络。

Conclusion: Wittstock包络为Hermitian完全有界映射提供了不依赖唯一绝对值的模与透视框架，其存在性与唯一性由中心支撑和Choi表示刻画，并推广了完全正映射的相关构造。

Abstract: A Hermitian completely bounded map $Φ:\mathscr{A}\to B(\mathcal{H})$
  need not have a canonical completely positive analogue of an absolute value. We study this problem through the ordered set $\mathsf{E}(Φ)$
  of completely positive symmetric majorants of $Φ$, which we call Wittstock envelopes.
  We show that every Wittstock envelope canonically determines a minimal Wittstock envelope below it. However, minimal envelopes need not be unique. We therefore study the stronger question of when a least completely positive majorant exists. We obtain a criterion, in terms of central supports of spectral projections, for the existence of least majorants below a fixed envelope. In finite dimensions, the Choi representation gives a simple characterization: a least completely positive majorant exists if and only if \(Φ\) or \(-Φ\) is completely positive.
  Finally, we use the envelope-relative modulus to define envelope-relative perspectives for Hermitian completely bounded maps. These constructions extend Okayasu's completely positive map perspectives, reduce to the usual perspective in the completely positive case, and remain dependent in general on the chosen Wittstock envelopes.

</details>


### [137] [A quasidiagonal $C^*$-algebra with a nonquasidiagonal maximal tensor square](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00230)
*Mehdi Moradi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 构造了一个可分、单位、剩余有限维的C*-代数A，使其极大张量平方包含真等距元，从而A是拟对角的而A⊗max A不是。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究C*-代数的拟对角性在张量积下是否保持，寻找反例：需要构造一个拟对角代数A使得其极大张量平方非拟对角（甚至含真等距元）。

Method: 用显式可数仿射群的同余商上的有限正则表示生成A；利用具有性质(T)的子群在极大张量平方中确定对角Kazhdan投影，通过膨胀的共轭作用使该投影等价于真子投影；用准正则表示检测严格性，其两个因子限制下降到A；给出下降所需的范数估计并显式构造真等距元。

Result: 得到A可分的单位剩余有限维C*-代数，A⊗max A含有真等距元；A拟对角但A⊗max A非拟对角；显式给出真等距元及范数估计。

Conclusion: 拟对角性在极大张量积下不保持，提供了A拟对角而A⊗max A非拟对角的例子。

Abstract: We construct a separable unital residually finite-dimensional $C^*$-algebra $A$ whose maximal tensor square contains a proper isometry. In particular, $A$ is quasidiagonal whereas $A\tmax A$ is not. The algebra is generated by finite regular representations obtained from congruence quotients of an explicit countable affine group. A property~$(T)$ subgroup determines a diagonal Kazhdan projection in the maximal tensor square. Conjugation by a dilation makes this projection equivalent to a proper subprojection. Strictness is detected by a quasi-regular representation whose two factor restrictions descend to the specified algebra $A$. We give the norm estimate needed for this descent and construct the proper isometry explicitly.

</details>


### [138] [An induction proof of strong NF\ for amenable group $C^*$-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00231)
*Mehdi Moradi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明可数离散顺从群的全群$C^*$-代数是强NF的，并通过表示论构造给出证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可数离散顺从群全群$C^*$-代数的结构分类性质，特别是其是否属于强NF代数这一重要归纳极限类。

Method: 利用以hyper-FC根为核、以极大ICC商为商的短正合列进行表示论构造；根的极端不变迹诱导群的因子表示；正则根迹的Choquet分解选出分离族；通过精确扭叉积商与序列UCT论证使像拟对角化；再用忠实不可约替换提供内拟对角性所需投影；并给出迹分解变体。

Result: 证明了可数离散顺从群的全群$C^*$-代数是强NF的；识别出两种证明共用的步骤。

Conclusion: 可数离散顺从群的全群$C^*$-代数具有强NF性质，该结论由表示论与迹分解两条路径共同支持。

Abstract: We give a proof that the full group $C^*$-algebra of a countable discrete amenable group is strong NF. The representation-theoretic construction uses the exact sequence whose kernel is the hyper-FC radical and whose quotient is the maximal ICC quotient. Extreme invariant traces on the radical induce factorial representations of the group. A Choquet decomposition of the regular radical trace selects a separating family whose induced traces are supported on the ordinary FC-centre. Exact twisted crossed-product quotients and a sequential UCT argument make their images quasidiagonal. Faithful irreducible replacements then supply the projections required for inner quasidiagonality. We also give a direct trace-decomposition variant and identify the steps shared by the two proofs.

</details>


### [139] [Gromov-Hausdorff Convergence of Spectral Truncations for Noncommutative Tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01132)
*Vibhor Bhatt,Satyajit Guin,Palash Sharma*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明了非交换 n-环面（n≥2）谱截断所对应算子系统的状态空间在 Connes 距离下满足 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛，并研究了截断算子系统的 C*-包络与传播数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典环面情形的收敛结果已有（文献[20]），本文旨在将其推广到非交换 n-环面的谱截断框架，并探究截断算子系统的结构性质在形变下是否保持。

Method: 利用 n-环面在非交换 n-环面上的作用，并结合 Bochner 积分的适当使用，构造一个 C^1-近似序同构来建立收敛性。

Result: 证明了非交换 n-环面谱截断算子系统状态空间（带 Connes 距离）的 Gromov-Hausdorff 收敛；经典环面情形作为特例成立。

Conclusion: 通过研究截断算子系统的 C*-包络和传播数，表明环面截断算子系统的结构性质在形变下得以保持。

Abstract: We prove the Gromov-Hausdorff convergence of the state spaces, equipped with Connes' distance function, of the operator systems associated with spectral truncations for noncommutative $n$-tori, $n\ge 2$. The action of the $n$-torus on the noncommutative $n$-torus, together with an appropriate use of Bochner integrals, enables us to construct an $C^1$-approximate order isomorphism that establishes the convergence. The earlier convergence result for the classical case of tori [20] follows as a special case of our result. We conclude by studying the $C^*$-envelopes and propagation numbers of the truncated operator systems and show that the structural properties of the truncated operator systems for tori persist under deformation.

</details>


### [140] [The crystal groupoid of a compact semisimple Lie group and its flag varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01208)
*Aidan Sims,Robert Yuncken*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文引入了紧半单李群 $K$ 的 crystal groupoid，将其构造为有限型子移位在高阶情形下于 Kashiwara crystal 逆向极限上的 Deaconu-Renault groupoid，并证明其 Steinberg 代数与 $C^*$-代数分别实现 $K$ 多项式函数代数的 crystal limits 及其 $C^*$-包络。进一步证明该 groupoid 的轨道空间与 $K$ 的 Weyl 群双射，其拓扑由 Weyl 群上的 Bruhat 序决定；并将构造推广到 flag manifold 与相关 Poisson 齐性空间，得到类似结构结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在高阶 Kashiwara crystal 逆向极限上建立类似有限型子移位的 groupoid 框架，从而在非交换几何/算子代数层面实现 $K$ 的多项式函数代数及其 $C^*$-包络的 crystal limits，并刻画其轨道空间与 Weyl 群、Bruhat 序的联系。

Method: 定义紧半单李群 $K$ 的 crystal groupoid，即 Kashiwara crystal 逆向极限上有限型子移位的高阶类比所对应的 Deaconu-Renault groupoid；通过其 Steinberg 代数与 $C^*$-代数实现 Matassa 与第二作者意义下的 crystal limits；分析轨道空间拓扑；并构造 flag manifold 及相关 Poisson 齐性空间的 crystal groupoid。

Result: Steinberg 代数与 $C^*$-代数分别实现 $K$ 多项式函数代数的 crystal limits 及其 $C^*$-envelope；crystal groupoid 的轨道空间与 $K$ 的 Weyl 群双射，拓扑由 Bruhat 序确定；对 flag manifold 及相关 Poisson 齐性空间也证明了类似结构结果。

Conclusion: crystal groupoid 为紧半单李群及其 flag manifold、Poisson 齐性空间提供统一的 groupoid/算子代数模型，将 crystal limits、Weyl 群轨道空间与 Bruhat 序拓扑联系起来。

Abstract: We introduce the crystal groupoid of a compact semisimple Lie group $K$; it is the Deaconu-Renault groupoid of a higher-rank analogue of a subshift of finite type on an inverse limit of Kashiwara crystals. We show that its Steinberg algebra and its $C^*$-algebra realise the crystal limits of the polynomial function algebra of $K$ and its $C^*$-envelope, respectively, in the sense of Matassa and the second author. We show that the orbit space of the crystal groupoid is in bijection with the Weyl group of $K$, with topology determined by the Bruhat order on the Weyl group. We also define a crystal groupoid for flag manifolds and related Poisson homogeneous spaces, and prove the analogous structural results.

</details>


### [141] [Free groups amenably act on unital simple AF-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01460)
*Yuhei Suzuki*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文证明自由群可在一元单AF-代数上实现顺从作用，从而解决了非顺从群在可分类单C*-代数上顺从作用存在性问题的最后一种情形，证明基于Baire纲定理，作者声明该结果获得GPT-6 Astra的大量协助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非顺从群在可分类单C*-代数上是否存在顺从作用是一个长期未决的存在性问题。此前多数情形已被解决，唯独自由群这一情形悬而未决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 证明主要依赖Baire纲定理，在一元单AF-代数上构造自由群的顺从作用。文中提及该结果在GPT-6 Astra的大量协助下获得。

Result: 证明了自由群在一元单AF-代数上存在顺从作用，为非顺从群在可分类单C*-代数上的顺从作用存在性问题提供了自由群情形下的肯定答案。

Conclusion: 自由群情形是存在性问题的最后一个未决情形，该结果完成了这一问题的全部情形，且体现了AI工具在数学研究中的实质性参与。

Abstract: We show that free groups admit amenable actions on unital simple AF-algebras. This settles, for free groups, the last remaining case of the existence problem for amenable actions of non-amenable groups on classifiable simple C*-algebras. The proof relies on the Baire category theorem. This result was obtained with substantial assistance from GPT-6 Astra.

</details>


### [142] [An Explicit Polynomial Counterexample to Connes' Embedding Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01536)
*Jiaqi Wang,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 构造显式Hermitian多项式f(65个自伴变量、次数12)，其归一化迹在任意维自伴矩阵压缩上都≥3/4，但在某群von Neumann代数的自伴酉元组上等于-1，从而给出Connes嵌入猜想代数形式的显式反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Connes嵌入猜想的代数形式断言：若多项式在矩阵逼近下取值非负，则在群von Neumann代数中也应非负。此前反例多为非构造性存在性结果，缺乏显式多项式，因此本文旨在给出一个完全显式、可写出的反例。

Method: 综合Kun–Thom的群构造、Thom的归一化论证以及Alekseev–Liu–Thom的谱修正定理，构造f=1-(PQ)^2+μΣ_{ν=1}^{825}E_ν^*E_ν+μΣ_{j=1}^{65}(1-X_j^2)^2，其中P,Q编码共轭对合，E_ν编码关系缺陷，μ为显式正整数；再通过归一化论证分离矩阵情形与群von Neumann代数情形。

Result: 对任意维度的自伴矩阵压缩元组，f的归一化迹≥3/4；而在某自伴酉元组上f=-1。因此对0≤ε<1，f+ε不属于模交换子的压缩二次模，构成Connes嵌入猜想代数形式的显式反例。

Conclusion: 显式Hermitian多项式f成功分离了矩阵逼近与群von Neumann代数行为，为Connes嵌入猜想代数形式提供了可验证的显式反例。

Abstract: We construct an explicit Hermitian polynomial $ f $ with integer coefficients, of degree $ 12 $ in $ 65 $ selfadjoint variables, whose normalized trace is at least $3/4$ on every tuple of selfadjoint matrix contractions, in every dimension, and equals $-1$ at a specified tuple of selfadjoint unitaries in a group von Neumann algebra. Consequently, $f+\varepsilon$ lies outside the contraction quadratic module modulo commutators for $0\le\varepsilon<1$, giving an explicit counterexample to the algebraic formulation of Connes' embedding conjecture. Combining the group construction of Kun and Thom with the normalization argument of Thom and the spectral correction theorem of Alekseev, Liu, and Thom, we determine an explicit positive integer $μ$ for which $f=1-(P Q)^2+μ\sum_{ν=1}^{825}E_ν^*E_ν+μ\sum_{j=1}^{65}(1-X_j^2)^2$. Here $P,Q$ encode conjugate involutions, and the $E_ν$ encode relation defects.

</details>


### [143] [A groupoid model for Rørdam's finite-infinite $C^*$-algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01543)
*Drieke Keuppens,Diego Martínez*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文为Rørdam的简单、核、可分离且同时含有限与无限投影的C*-代数例子构造了一个groupoid模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rørdam原始构造中的归纳极限缺乏几何/groupoid描述，作者希望通过构造合适的groupoid模型，为这类性质特殊的C*-代数提供更具体的刻画，并探究amenability与comparison之间的关系。

Method: 选取一种精心设计的归纳极限构造的模型（其交叉积），构造对应的groupoid模型，并证明该构造给出极限的Cartan子代数；由此得到局部紧、第二可数、Hausdorff、étale、amenable、极小且拓扑主部的groupoid。

Result: 所构造groupoid的单位空间紧致，其C*-代数同时包含一个无限投影和一个非零有限投影。

Conclusion: 该groupoid是amenable的，但不具有comparison性质，从而提供了amenability不蕴含comparison的一个实例。

Abstract: We construct a groupoid model for Rordam's example of a simple, nuclear, separable $C^*$-algebra containing a finite and an infinite projection. This groupoid model arises from a (crossed product of a) carefully chosen model for the inductive limit construction in Rordam's original approach, which we show to yield a Cartan subalgebra of the limit. In particular, this procedure yields a locally compact, second countable, Hausdorff, étale, amenable, minimal and topologically principal groupoid whose unit space is compact, and whose $C^*$-algebra contains an infinite and a non-zero finite projection. Hence, this groupoid is amenable, and yet does not have comparison.

</details>


### [144] [On C*-nuclearity of graph operator systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01676)
*Adam Dor-On,Travis Russell*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文完全刻画了图算子系统为C*-核的有限简单无向图：当且仅当该图是2-弦图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图算子系统是算子空间理论中的重要研究对象，其C*-核性（C*-nuclearity）刻画了该系统与C*-代数的关系。此前缺乏对哪些图的算子系统具有C*-核性的完整分类。

Method: 通过对有限简单无向图进行分类分析，将其图算子系统的C*-核性与图论性质联系起来，利用McCullough定义的2-弦图（2-chordal）作为判别准则进行证明。

Result: 证明了图算子系统为C*-核当且仅当对应图为2-弦图；给出了首个非弦图但其图算子系统为C*-核的例子，以及首批图算子系统非C*-核的例子。

Conclusion: 图算子系统的C*-核性由图的2-弦性完整决定，这一分类揭示了图结构与算子系统核性之间的精确对应关系。

Abstract: We classify the set of finite simple undirected graphs whose corresponding graph operator system is C*-nuclear. We prove that the operator system associated to the graph is C*-nuclear if and only if the graph is 2-chordal, as defined by McCullough. In particular, we present the first known example of a non-chordal graph whose graph operator system is C*-nuclear, as well as the first known examples of graph operator systems which are not C*-nuclear.

</details>


### [145] [The Baum-Connes conjecture: a concise course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01802)
*Alain Valette*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 这是作者于2025年1-2月在庞加莱研究所（IHP）"表示论与非交换几何"主题学期中讲授的10小时课程的讲义（经轻微编辑）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原文仅说明材料来源（IHP主题学期课程讲义），未提供研究动机。

Method: 原文未说明具体方法。

Result: 原文未报告任何结果。

Conclusion: 原文未给出结论；如需分析实质内容，请提供摘要正文或更长文本。

Abstract: These are the slightly edited notes from a 10-hour course I taught at Institut Henri Poincaré in January-February 2025 in the framework of the thematic semester "Representation theory and noncommutative geometry".

</details>


### [146] [The Cuntz semigroup of a unital graph C*-algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01915)
*Yao Ming Chan*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文计算了含单位图C*-代数的Cuntz半群，并研究其性质，表明在该半群层面不存在图C*-代数核维数至多为1的猜想的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算图C*-代数的Cuntz半群有助于理解其结构性质，并检验关于核维数的猜想是否存在障碍。

Method: 结合Hong与Szymański对图C*-代数整个理想格的实现、Ara、Moreno与Pardo对含单位图C*-代数Murray-von Neumann半群的计算，以及多种Cuntz半群技巧。

Result: 给出了含单位图C*-代数Cuntz半群的计算，并研究了该类Cuntz半群的若干性质。

Conclusion: 在Cuntz半群层面，不存在任何图C*-代数核维数至多为1这一猜想的障碍。

Abstract: We compute the Cuntz semigroup of a unital graph C*-algebra by combining Hong and Szymański's realisation of the entire ideal lattice of a graph C*-algebra, Ara, Moreno and Pardo's computation of the Murray-von Neumann semigroup of a unital graph C*-algebra and various Cuntz semigroup techniques. We also investigate certain properties of such Cuntz semigroups, and demonstrate that at the level of the Cuntz semigroup, there is no obstruction to the conjecture that the nuclear dimension of any graph C*-algebra is at most one.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum real projective spaces as quantum CW-complexes via topological graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02017)
*Atul Gothe*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 利用拓扑图构造量子实射影空间的量子CW复形，并引入多重拉回偶球作为非交换"圆柱"的边界，证明其具有类似将球粘合到中空闭圆柱两端的粘合结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情形下实射影空间可通过CW复形构造，本文旨在非交换几何框架下建立相应的量子CW复形理论，将此构造推广至量子实射影空间。

Method: 借助拓扑图的概念与性质构造量子CW复形；引入多重拉回偶球（multipullback even-spheres）作为非交换"圆柱"的边界。

Result: 成功构造出量子实射影空间的量子CW复形，其构造方式与经典CW复形构造相对应；并证明多重拉回偶球具有类似于将球粘合到中空闭圆柱端部的粘合结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子实射影空间提供了CW复形式的组合描述，并通过多重拉回偶球的粘合性质，建立了非交换几何与经典拓扑构造之间的类比。

Abstract: We use the notion and properties of topological graphs to construct a quantum CW complex for quantum real projective spaces. This construction mirrors the CW complex construction in the classical case. Moreover, we introduce multipullback even-spheres as boundaries of noncommutative "cylinders" and show that they admit a gluing akin to the gluing of balls to the ends of a hollow closed cylinder.

</details>


### [148] [Unital embeddings of the Jiang--Su algebra are not unique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02174)
*Matteo Pagliero*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 证明了Jiang–Su代数到自由积中的典范嵌入不近似酉等价，但在具有唯一迹严格比较的单纯单调迹C*-代数中任意两个此类嵌入是强渐近酉等价的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Schafhauser、Tikuisis与White所列99个问题中的一个，厘清Jiang–Su代数嵌入在何种意义下（强渐近酉等价 vs 近似酉等价）可以进行分类。

Method: 利用强渐近酉等价与近似酉等价之间的区分，分别在自由积环境与具有唯一迹严格比较的单调迹C*-代数环境中分析典范嵌入。反例方面构造不可近似酉等价的两个典范嵌入；正面结果利用迹与严格比较的性质证明强渐近酉等价。

Result: （1）Jiang–Su代数到universal unital free product Z *_C Z 的典范嵌入不近似酉等价；（2）到任何单纯、含单位、单调迹且关于唯一迹满足严格比较的C*-代数中的任意两个此类嵌入是强渐近酉等价的。

Conclusion: 在一般情况下（如自由积）近似酉等价失效，但在具唯一迹严格比较的单调迹C*-代数中，嵌入分类可由更弱的强渐近酉等价恢复，说明近似酉等价与强渐近酉等价在此类问题中的区别至关重要。

Abstract: We show that the canonical embeddings of the Jiang--Su algebra $\mathcal Z$ into the universal unital free product $\mathcal Z \ast_{\mathbb C}\mathcal Z$ are not approximately unitarily equivalent. This answers one of the $99$ problems in the list of Schafhauser, Tikuisis, and White. By contrast, we prove that any two such embeddings into a simple, unital, monotracial $\mathrm{C}^\ast$-algebra with strict comparison by the unique trace are strongly asymptotically unitarily equivalent.

</details>


<div id='math.CA'></div>

# math.CA [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Stability of concentration in the Paley-Wiener space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01299)
*Denis Zelent*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了在固定时间-带宽乘积下，Paley-Wiener 空间中能量集中度的稳定性：集中度亏损可控制与平移后第一长球面波函数的平方 L² 距离，以及与同中心区间的平方归一化对称差，且函数距离的指数最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长球面波函数是时间-带宽集中问题的极值函数，已有定量不确定性原理刻画了最大集中度。但集中度接近最优时，对应的函数是否必须"接近"某个平移的第一长球面波函数，以及这种稳定性以何种最优速率成立，此前缺乏精确结果。本文旨在给出这类集中度稳定的定量描述并确定最优指数。

Method: 核心技巧是引入以最优优化子（best optimizers）加权的集合差异（set discrepancy）来替代通常的对称差度量，避免直接处理对称差带来的技术困难。证明将初等 Hilbert 空间论证与长球面波函数的经典性质相结合，对固定时间-带宽乘积逐一定量分析。

Result: 对每个固定时间-带宽乘积，集中度亏损控制了与某个平移的第一长球面波函数之间的平方 L² 距离，以及与该平移对应同中心区间的平方归一化对称差；所得到的函数距离指数是最优的。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 Paley-Wiener 空间中集中度稳定性的精确刻画，表明集中度亏损与最优函数之间的接近程度具有最优幂次关系，从而完善了定量不确定性原理在极端集中情形下的稳定性理论。

Abstract: We prove stability of concentration in the Paley-Wiener space for each fixed time-bandwidth product. The concentration deficit controls the squared $L^2(\mathbb R)$-distance from a translated first prolate spheroidal wave function and the squared normalized symmetric difference from an interval at the same center. The obtained exponent for the function distance is optimal. The main idea is the introduction of the set discrepancy weighted by the best optimizers in place of the symmetric difference. The proof then combines elementary Hilbert space arguments with classical properties of the prolate spheroidal wave functions.

</details>


### [150] [Strip Decoupling Inequalities for the Paraboloid in $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01567)
*Jacob Glidewell*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中 strips（抛物面 $R^{-1}$-邻域按 $1\times R^{-1/2}\times R^{-1}$ 曲面大帽的分割）的 sharp $\ell^2(L^{10/3})$ 解耦估计，方法是将 strip 解耦不等式化归为平面关联问题，并用双端 Furstenberg 关联估计处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大帽或大集合解耦不等式最初由 Demeter 研究，与 Fourier 限制理论中的反向平方函数猜想和限制猜想密切相关。本文关注 strips 这一特定分割下的解耦问题，旨在建立其 sharp 估计。

Method: 将 strip 解耦不等式约化为平面关联问题（planar incidence problem），并借助双端 Furstenberg 关联估计（two-ends Furstenberg incidence estimates）加以解决。方法受 Wang--Wu 关于限制猜想工作的启发。

Result: 证明了 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 中 strips 的 sharp $\ell^2(L^{10/3})$ 解耦估计。

Conclusion: 本文建立了 strips 的 sharp $\ell^2(L^{10/3})$ 解耦估计，通过平面关联问题与双端 Furstenberg 关联估计实现了约化，为 Fourier 限制理论中相关猜想的研究提供了新的工具与思路。

Abstract: Large-cap or large-ensemble decoupling inequalities were initially studied by Demeter. These are intimately connected to the reverse square-function conjecture and restriction conjecture in Fourier restriction theory. By strips, we mean a partition of the $R^{-1}$-neighborhood of the paraboloid into $1\times R^{-1/2}\times R^{-1}$ curved large-caps. In this article, we prove the sharp $\ell^2(L^{10/3})$ decoupling estimate for strips in $\mathbb{R}^3$. We reduce the strip decoupling inequalities to a planar incidence problem, which is dealt with via two-ends Furstenberg incidence estimates. Our method is inspired by Wang--Wu on the restriction conjecture.

</details>


### [151] [Quantitative possibilities for Baxter's theorem in nonlinear Fourier analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01711)
*Michel Alexis,Gevorg Mnatsakanyan,Kristina Oganesyan*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究单位圆上正交多项式Baxter定理的定量版本，重点针对SU(2)值非线性Fourier变换（NLFT），构造数论反例证明定量范数与Lipschitz估计不成立，并给出一个正结果及对Baxter方法局限性的讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Baxter定理指出单位圆上测度的线性Fourier系数属于ℓ¹当且仅当Verblunsky系数属于ℓ¹，但该等价性仅为定性结论，未给出范数与Lipschitz意义下的定量控制。近期在SU(2)值NLFT上获得了Baxter定理的类比，因此作者希望探究能否建立相应的定量定理，并厘清其可行边界。

Method: 针对SU(2)值非线性Fourier变换，尝试建立带范数估计与Lipschitz估计的定量Baxter型定理；核心手段是一个数论构造的反例，用以否定若干定量命题；同时给出一个正结果，并分析经典Baxter方法在这一框架下的适用限度。

Result: 通过数论构造证明NLFT的若干定量方向存在负面结果（相应范数/Lipschitz估计不成立），并得到一个正面结果；同时阐明Baxter原有方法的局限。

Conclusion: Baxter定理的定性等价性在SU(2)值NLFT框架下不能直接提升为一般定量估计，定量版本受数论构造制约，仅在特定条件下成立；经典Baxter方法不足以处理该问题。

Abstract: Baxter's classical theorem for orthogonal polynomials on the unit circle establishes that the linear Fourier coefficients of a measure are in $\ell^1$ if and only if the nonlinear coefficients, i.e., the so-called Verblunsky coefficients, are in $\ell^1$. However, this equivalence is purely qualitative. We explore possible formulations of such quantitative theorems with norm and Lipschitz estimates for the $SU(2)$-valued nonlinear Fourier transform, for which the analog of Baxter's theorem has been recently obtained. In particular, the highlight of this paper is a number-theoretic construction for the NLFT which proves some negative results in this direction. We also prove a positive result and discuss the limitations of Baxter's method.

</details>


### [152] [Positive Bidiagonal Factorizations: Combinatorial Structure, Spectral Theory, and Matrix Continued Fractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26066)
*Manuel Mañas*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文研究半无限带状矩阵的指定正双对角分解，揭示其谱、逼近与可积结构：建立循环置换下的归一化LU正分解、Christoffel字与最小高度重构、星形谱与径向Stieltjes矩，并给出矩阵连分式的Padé型逼近、单调收敛及与混合型Favard/Weyl理论的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半无限带状矩阵的双对角（Darboux）分解与正分解在正交多项式、Favard理论、可积系统中有重要意义，但对任意上下带宽的带状矩阵，其正分解的谱结构、逼近性质与可积层级尚缺乏统一刻画。作者希望把循环Darboux轨道、Christoffel变换与混合型Favard理论、径向矩问题和稀疏Lax层级联系起来。

Method: 对半无限带状矩阵的因子作循环置换，构造归一化下-上（LU）正分解，并证明在适当次数条件下因子转移诱导矩阵Christoffel变换。用Christoffel字刻画最小高度正重构及其循环乘积，分析相应的两类块位移（互素带宽时退化为双对角）矩阵。结合平面网络生成正径向Stieltjes矩序列，研究紧径向情形下星支撑表示测度的原点与负分数矩条件。对保持因子次序的情形，构造矩阵连分式，给出Padé型接触、分母分解、后向求值与误差界，并在Favard识别下逼近混合型Weyl矩阵。

Result: 循环置换下存在归一化LU正分解，因子转移对应Christoffel变换，将循环Darboux轨道与混合型Favard理论相连。Christoffel字给出最小高度正重构与达到该高度的循环乘积；相应矩阵具两类块位移，互素带宽时化为双对角，其有限非零谱位于星形集合上，平面网络产生正径向Stieltjes矩序列。紧径向情形下，星支撑表示测度由原点条件和负分数矩条件刻画。互素带宽下径向矩形变给出稀疏Lax层级的行列式解，并同步循环Toda流。保持因子次序时得到矩阵连分式，具显式Padé型接触、分母分解、后向求值与误差界；有界非负因子时收敛子单调收敛于容许域内最小非负解，并在Favard识别下逼近混合型Weyl矩阵；无界分解保持形式逼近，解析收敛需闭实现与稳定性假设。

Conclusion: 该工作以指定正双对角分解为统一框架，把循环Darboux轨道、Christoffel字、星形谱、径向Stieltjes矩、稀疏Lax层级与循环Toda流以及矩阵连分式逼近整合起来。其核心价值在于对任意上下带宽建立谱-逼近-可积三位一体的结构，并在Favard识别下刻画混合型Weyl矩阵的逼近；应用涵盖Piñeiro系统与类Jacobi系统，在给定正性与AT假设下给出全局正系数可积解。

Abstract: Prescribed positive bidiagonal factorizations of semi-infinite banded matrices reveal spectral, approximation, and integrable structures for arbitrary lower and upper bandwidths. Every cyclic permutation of the factors admits a normalized lower--upper positive factorization. Under suitable degree conditions, factor transfers induce matrix Christoffel transformations, linking the cyclic Darboux orbit to mixed-type Favard theory.
  Christoffel words determine minimum-height positive refactorizations and the cyclic products that reach them. The associated matrices have two block displacements, reducing to two diagonals for coprime bandwidths. Their finite nonzero spectra lie on a star, and planar networks yield positive radial Stieltjes moment sequences. In the compact radial case star-supported representing measures are characterized by conditions at the origin and negative fractional moments. For coprime bandwidths, a radial moment deformation gives determinant solutions of a sparse Lax hierarchy and synchronizes the cyclic Toda flows.
  Retaining the prescribed factor order yields matrix continued fractions with explicit Padé-type contact, denominator factorizations, backward evaluation, and error bounds. For bounded nonnegative factors, the convergents are monotone and converge to the least nonnegative solution in the admissible domain. Under the Favard identification, they approximate the mixed-type Weyl matrix. Unbounded factorizations retain the formal approximation; analytic convergence requires a closed realization and stability hypotheses, with an additional identification for measure-defined Weyl matrices. Piñeiro and Jacobi-like systems give applications, including global positive coefficientwise integrable solutions under the stated positivity and AT hypotheses.

</details>
